namespace DVLD_DataAccess
{
    public class clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationDataAccess {

        public static int AddNewLocalDrivingLicenseApplication(LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationDTO LocalDrivingLicenseApplication)
        {
            int LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID = -1;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = @"INSERT INTO LocalDrivingLicenseApplications (ApplicationID, LicenseClassID)
                             VALUES (@ApplicationID, @LicenseClassID);
                             SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY();";
            try
            {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@ApplicationID", LocalDrivingLicenseApplication.ApplicationID);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@LicenseClassID", LocalDrivingLicenseApplication.LicenseClassID);

                object result = command.ExecuteScalar();

                if (result != null && int.TryParse(result.ToString(), out int insertedID))
                    LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID = insertedID;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }
                
            
            return LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID;
        }

        public static LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationDTO? GetLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfoByID(int LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID)
        {

            LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationDTO? dto = null;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = @"SELECT LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID, ApplicationID, LicenseClassID
                             FROM LocalDrivingLicenseApplications
                             WHERE LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID = @LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID";

            try
            {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID", LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID);

                using SqlDataReader reader = command.ExecuteReader();

                if (reader.Read())
                    dto = new LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationDTO(reader.GetInt32(reader.GetOrdinal("LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID")), reader.GetInt32(reader.GetOrdinal("ApplicationID")), reader.GetInt32(reader.GetOrdinal("LicenseClassID")));

            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }

            return dto;
        }

        public static bool UpdateLocalDrivingLicenseApplication(LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationDTO dto)
        {

            int RowsAffected = 0;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = @"UPDATE LocalDrivingLicenseApplications
                             SET ApplicationID = @ApplicationID, LicenseClassID = @LicenseClassID
                             WHERE LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID = @LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID";

            try
            {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@ApplicationID", dto.ApplicationID);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@LicenseClassID", dto.LicenseClassID);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID", dto.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID);

                RowsAffected = command.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }

            return (RowsAffected > 0);
        }

        public static LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationDTO? GetLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfoByPersonID(int PersonID)
        {
            LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationDTO? dto = null;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = @"SELECT L.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID, L.ApplicationID, L.LicenseClassID
                             FROM LocalDrivingLicenseApplications L
                             INNER JOIN Applications A ON L.ApplicationID = A.ApplicationID
                             WHERE A.ApplicantPersonID = @PersonID";
            try
            {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@PersonID", PersonID);

                using SqlDataReader reader = command.ExecuteReader();

                if (reader.Read())
                    dto = new LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationDTO(reader.GetInt32(reader.GetOrdinal("LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID")), reader.GetInt32(reader.GetOrdinal("ApplicationID")), reader.GetInt32(reader.GetOrdinal("LicenseClassID")));
            
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }
            return dto;
        }

        public static DataTable GetAllLocalDrivingLicenseApplications()
        {
            DataTable dt = new DataTable();
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = @"SELECT LDLA.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID, LC.ClassName, P.NationalNo, P.FirstName + ' ' + P.SecondName + ' ' + ISNULL(P.ThirdName, '') + ' ' + P.LastName AS FullName, A.ApplicationDate, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM Tests T INNER JOIN TestAppointments TA ON T.TestAppointmentID = TA.TestAppointmentID WHERE TA.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID = LDLA.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID AND T.TestResult = 1) AS PassedTests, A.ApplicationStatus, A.ApplicantPersonID
                             FROM LocalDrivingLicenseApplications LDLA
                             INNER JOIN Applications A ON LDLA.ApplicationID = A.ApplicationID
                             INNER JOIN LicenseClasses LC ON LDLA.LicenseClassID = LC.LicenseClassID
                             INNER JOIN People P ON A.ApplicantPersonID = P.PersonID
                             ORDER BY LDLA.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID DESC";
            try {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                using SqlDataReader reader = command.ExecuteReader();

                if (reader.HasRows)
                    dt.Load(reader);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }

            return dt;
        }

        public static bool DoesActiveApplicationExist(int PersonID, int LicenseClassID) {
            
            bool IsExist = false;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = @"SELECT 1
                             FROM LocalDrivingLicenseApplications L
                             INNER JOIN Applications A ON L.ApplicationID = A.ApplicationID
                             WHERE A.ApplicantPersonID = @PersonID
                             AND L.LicenseClassID = @LicenseClassID
                             AND A.ApplicationStatus IN (1, 3)";

            try {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@PersonID", PersonID);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@LicenseClassID", LicenseClassID);

                object result = command.ExecuteScalar();
                
                IsExist = (result != null);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }

            return IsExist;
        }

        public static bool DeleteLocalDrivingLicenseApplication(int LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID)
        {
            int RowsAffected = 0;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);

            string query = @"DELETE FROM LocalDrivingLicenseApplications 
                             WHERE LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID = @LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID";

            try {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID", LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID);

                RowsAffected = command.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }

            return (RowsAffected > 0);
        }

        public static int GetPassedTestsCount(int LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID)
        {
            int PassedTests = 0;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = @"SELECT COUNT(*) 
                     FROM Tests T 
                     INNER JOIN TestAppointments TA ON T.TestAppointmentID = TA.TestAppointmentID 
                     WHERE TA.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID = @LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID 
                       AND T.TestResult = 1";

            try
            {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID", LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID);

                object result = command.ExecuteScalar();

                if (result != null && int.TryParse(result.ToString(), out int count))
                    PassedTests = count;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }

            return PassedTests;
        }

    }
}
﻿using DVLD_DTOs;
using System;
using System.Data;
using System.Data.SqlClient;

namespace DVLD_DataAccess {
    public class clsPersonDataAccess {

        public static DataTable GetAllPeople() {
            DataTable dt = new DataTable();

            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = "SELECT * FROM People";

            try {
                con.Open();

                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);

                using SqlDataReader reader = command.ExecuteReader();

                if (reader.HasRows) {
                    dt.Load(reader);
                }

            } catch (Exception ex) {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }

            return dt;
        }

        public static int AddNewPerson(PersonDTO PersonInfo) {
            int PersonID = -1;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = @"INSERT INTO People (FirstName, SecondName, ThirdName, LastName, NationalNo, DateOfBirth, Gendor, Address, Phone, Email, NationalityCountryID, ImagePath)
                             VALUES (@FirstName, @SecondName, @ThirdName, @LastName, @NationalNo, @DateOfBirth, @Gendor, @Address, @Phone, @Email, @NationalityCountryID, @ImagePath);
                             SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY();";

            try {
                con.Open();

                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@FirstName", PersonInfo.FirstName);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@SecondName", PersonInfo.SecondName);

                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PersonInfo.ThirdName))
                    command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@ThirdName", DBNull.Value);
                else
                    command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@ThirdName", PersonInfo.ThirdName);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@LastName", PersonInfo.LastName);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@NationalNo", PersonInfo.NationalNo);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@DateOfBirth", PersonInfo.DateOfBirth);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Gendor", PersonInfo.Gendor);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Address", PersonInfo.Address);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Phone", PersonInfo.Phone);
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PersonInfo.Email))
                    command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Email", DBNull.Value);
                else
                    command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Email", PersonInfo.Email);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@NationalityCountryID", PersonInfo.NationalityCountryID);

                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PersonInfo.ImagePath))
                    command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@ImagePath", DBNull.Value);
                else
                    command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@ImagePath", PersonInfo.ImagePath);

                object result = command.ExecuteScalar();

                if (result != null && int.TryParse(result.ToString(), out int insertedID))
                    PersonID = insertedID;

            } catch (Exception ex) {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }
            return PersonID;
        }

        public static bool IsPersonExist(int PersonID) {
            bool IsFound = false;

            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = "SELECT found = 1 FROM People WHERE PersonID = @PersonID";

            try {
                con.Open();

                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@PersonID", PersonID);

                object result = command.ExecuteScalar();

                if (result != null)
                    IsFound = true;


            } catch (Exception ex) {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }

            return IsFound;
        }

        public static bool IsPersonExist(string NationalNo) {
            bool IsFound = false;

            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = "SELECT found = 1 FROM People WHERE NationalNo = @NationalNo";

            try {
                con.Open();

                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@NationalNo", NationalNo);

                object result = command.ExecuteScalar();

                if (result != null)
                    IsFound = true;


            } catch (Exception ex) {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }

            return IsFound;
        }

        public static bool DeletePerson(int PersonID) {
            bool IsDeleted = false;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = "DELETE FROM People WHERE PersonID = @PersonID";

            try {
                con.Open();

                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@PersonID", PersonID);

                int RowsAffected = command.ExecuteNonQuery();

                if (RowsAffected > 0)
                    IsDeleted = true;



            } catch (Exception ex) {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }

            return IsDeleted;
        }

        public static bool UpdatePerson(PersonDTO PersonInfo) {

            bool IsUpdated = false;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = @"UPDATE People
                             SET FirstName = @FirstName, SecondName = @SecondName, ThirdName = @ThirdName, LastName = @LastName, NationalNo = @NationalNo, DateOfBirth = @DateOfBirth, Gendor = @Gendor, Address = @Address, Phone = @Phone, Email = @Email, NationalityCountryID = @NationalityCountryID, ImagePath = @ImagePath
                             WHERE PersonID = @PersonID";

            try {
                con.Open();

                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@PersonID", PersonInfo.PersonID);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@FirstName", PersonInfo.FirstName);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@SecondName", PersonInfo.SecondName);

                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PersonInfo.ThirdName))
                    command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@ThirdName", DBNull.Value);
                else
                    command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@ThirdName", PersonInfo.ThirdName);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@LastName", PersonInfo.LastName);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@NationalNo", PersonInfo.NationalNo);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@DateOfBirth", PersonInfo.DateOfBirth);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Gendor", PersonInfo.Gendor);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Address", PersonInfo.Address);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Phone", PersonInfo.Phone);

                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PersonInfo.Email))
                    command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Email", DBNull.Value);
                else
                    command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Email", PersonInfo.Email);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@NationalityCountryID", PersonInfo.NationalityCountryID);

                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PersonInfo.ImagePath))
                    command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@ImagePath", DBNull.Value);
                else
                    command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@ImagePath", PersonInfo.ImagePath);

                int RowsAffected = command.ExecuteNonQuery();

                if (RowsAffected > 0)
                    IsUpdated = true;


            } catch (Exception ex) {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }

            return IsUpdated;
        }

        public static PersonDTO? GetPersonInfoByID(int PersonID) {

            PersonDTO? PersonInfo = null;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = "SELECT * FROM People WHERE PersonID = @PersonID";

            try {
                con.Open();

                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@PersonID", PersonID);

                using SqlDataReader reader = command.ExecuteReader();

                if(reader.Read())
                    PersonInfo = new PersonDTO(reader.GetInt32(reader.GetOrdinal("PersonID")), reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("NationalNo")), reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("FirstName")), reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("SecondName")), reader.IsDBNull(reader.GetOrdinal("ThirdName")) ? null : reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("ThirdName")), reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("LastName")), reader.GetDateTime(reader.GetOrdinal("DateOfBirth")), reader.GetByte(reader.GetOrdinal("Gendor")), reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("Address")), reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("Phone")), reader.IsDBNull(reader.GetOrdinal("Email")) ? null : reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("Email")), reader.GetInt32(reader.GetOrdinal("NationalityCountryID")), reader.IsDBNull(reader.GetOrdinal("ImagePath")) ? null : reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("ImagePath")));


            } catch (Exception ex) {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }
            return PersonInfo;
        }

        public static PersonDTO? GetPersonInfoByNationalNo(string NationalNo) {

            PersonDTO? PersonInfo = null;

            string query = "SELECT * FROM People WHERE NationalNo = @NationalNo";

                con.Open();

                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@NationalNo", NationalNo);

                using SqlDataReader reader = command.ExecuteReader();

                if (reader.Read())
                    PersonInfo = new PersonDTO(reader.GetInt32(reader.GetOrdinal("PersonID")), reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("NationalNo")), reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("FirstName")), reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("SecondName")), reader.IsDBNull(reader.GetOrdinal("ThirdName")) ? null : reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("ThirdName")), reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("LastName")), reader.GetDateTime(reader.GetOrdinal("DateOfBirth")), reader.GetByte(reader.GetOrdinal("Gendor")), reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("Address")), reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("Phone")), reader.IsDBNull(reader.GetOrdinal("Email")) ? null : reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("Email")), reader.GetInt32(reader.GetOrdinal("NationalityCountryID")), reader.IsDBNull(reader.GetOrdinal("ImagePath")) ? null : reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("ImagePath")));


            } catch (Exception ex) {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }
            return PersonInfo;
        }

        public static DataTable GetAllCountries() {
            DataTable dt = new DataTable();

        }


        public static string GetCountryNameByID(int CountryID) {
            string CountryName = "N/A";
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = "SELECT CountryName FROM Countries WHERE CountryID = @CountryID";

            try {
                SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@CountryID", CountryID);
                con.Open();

                object result = command.ExecuteScalar();
                
                if (result != null)
                    CountryName = result.ToString();
                
            } catch(Exception e)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(e);
            }

            return CountryName;
        }

﻿using DVLD_DTOs;
using System;
using System.Data;
using System.Data.SqlClient;

namespace DVLD_DataAccess
{
    public class clsTestAppointmentDataAccess
    {
        public static TestAppointmentDTO? GetTestAppointmentInfoByID(int TestAppointmentID)
        {
            TestAppointmentDTO? dto = null;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);

            string query = @"SELECT TestAppointmentID, TestTypeID, LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID, 
                            AppointmentDate, PaidFees, CreatedByUserID, IsLocked, RetakeTestApplicationID 
                     FROM TestAppointments 
                     WHERE TestAppointmentID = @TestAppointmentID";

            try
            {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@TestAppointmentID", TestAppointmentID);

                using SqlDataReader reader = cmd.ExecuteReader();
                if (reader.Read())
                {
                    int RetakeTestApplicationID = reader.IsDBNull(reader.GetOrdinal("RetakeTestApplicationID"))
                        ? -1
                        : reader.GetInt32(reader.GetOrdinal("RetakeTestApplicationID"));

                    dto = new TestAppointmentDTO(reader.GetInt32(reader.GetOrdinal("TestAppointmentID")), reader.GetInt32(reader.GetOrdinal("TestTypeID")), reader.GetInt32(reader.GetOrdinal("LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID")), reader.GetDateTime(reader.GetOrdinal("AppointmentDate")), reader.GetDecimal(reader.GetOrdinal("PaidFees")), reader.GetInt32(reader.GetOrdinal("CreatedByUserID")), reader.GetBoolean(reader.GetOrdinal("IsLocked")), RetakeTestApplicationID );
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }
            return dto;
        }
        
        public static bool LockTestAppointment(int TestAppointmentID)
        {
            int rowsAffected = 0;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = "UPDATE TestAppointments SET IsLocked = 1 WHERE TestAppointmentID = @TestAppointmentID";

            try
            {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@TestAppointmentID", TestAppointmentID);
                rowsAffected = cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }
            return (rowsAffected > 0);
        }

        public static int GetTotalTrialsPerTest(int LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID, int TestTypeID)
        {
            int TotalTrials = 0;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);

            string query = @"SELECT COUNT(*) 
                     FROM TestAppointments 
                     WHERE LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID = @LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID 
                       AND TestTypeID = @TestTypeID
                       AND IsLocked = 1"; 

            try
            {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID", LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@TestTypeID", TestTypeID);

                object result = command.ExecuteScalar();
                if (result != null && int.TryParse(result.ToString(), out int trials))
                {
                    TotalTrials = trials;
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }

            return TotalTrials;
        }

        public static int AddNewTestAppointment(TestAppointmentDTO dto)
        {
            int TestAppointmentID = -1;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);

            string query = @"INSERT INTO TestAppointments 
                     (TestTypeID, LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID, AppointmentDate, PaidFees, CreatedByUserID, IsLocked, RetakeTestApplicationID) 
                     VALUES (@TestTypeID, @LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID, @AppointmentDate, @PaidFees, @CreatedByUserID, @IsLocked, @RetakeTestApplicationID);
                     SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY();";
            try
            {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@TestTypeID", dto.TestTypeID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID", dto.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@AppointmentDate", dto.AppointmentDate);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@PaidFees", dto.PaidFees);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@CreatedByUserID", dto.CreatedByUserID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@IsLocked", dto.IsLocked);

                if (dto.RetakeTestApplicationID != -1)
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@RetakeTestApplicationID", dto.RetakeTestApplicationID);
                else
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@RetakeTestApplicationID", DBNull.Value);

                object result = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
                if (result != null && int.TryParse(result.ToString(), out int insertedID))
                {
                    TestAppointmentID = insertedID;
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }
            return TestAppointmentID;
        }

        public static bool UpdateTestAppointment(TestAppointmentDTO dto)
        {
            int rowsAffected = 0;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);

            string query = @"UPDATE TestAppointments 
                     SET TestTypeID = @TestTypeID, 
                         LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID = @LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID, 
                         AppointmentDate = @AppointmentDate, 
                         PaidFees = @PaidFees, 
                         CreatedByUserID = @CreatedByUserID, 
                         IsLocked = @IsLocked, 
                         RetakeTestApplicationID = @RetakeTestApplicationID
                     WHERE TestAppointmentID = @TestAppointmentID";
            try
            {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@TestAppointmentID", dto.TestAppointmentID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@TestTypeID", dto.TestTypeID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID", dto.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@AppointmentDate", dto.AppointmentDate);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@PaidFees", dto.PaidFees);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@CreatedByUserID", dto.CreatedByUserID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@IsLocked", dto.IsLocked);

                if (dto.RetakeTestApplicationID != -1)
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@RetakeTestApplicationID", dto.RetakeTestApplicationID);
                else
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@RetakeTestApplicationID", DBNull.Value);

                rowsAffected = cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }
            return (rowsAffected > 0);
        }

        public static DataTable GetAllTestAppointmentsByLocalAppID(int LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID, int TestTypeID)
        {
            DataTable dt = new DataTable();
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = @"SELECT TestAppointmentID, AppointmentDate, PaidFees, IsLocked
                     FROM TestAppointments
                     WHERE LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID = @LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID
                       AND TestTypeID = @TestTypeID
                     ORDER BY AppointmentDate DESC";

            try
            {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID", LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@TestTypeID", TestTypeID);

                using SqlDataReader reader = cmd.ExecuteReader();
                if (reader.HasRows)
                    dt.Load(reader);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }

            return dt;
        }

    }
}﻿using DVLD_DTOs;
using System;
using System.Data.SqlClient;

namespace DVLD_DataAccess
{
    public class clsTestDataAccess
    {
        public static int AddNewTest(TestDTO Test)
        {
            int TestID = -1;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = @"INSERT INTO Tests (TestAppointmentID, TestResult, Notes, CreatedByUserID)
                             VALUES (@TestAppointmentID, @TestResult, @Notes, @CreatedByUserID);
                             SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY();";
            try
            {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@TestAppointmentID", Test.TestAppointmentID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@TestResult", Test.TestResult);

                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Test.Notes))
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Notes", DBNull.Value);
                else
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Notes", Test.Notes);

                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@CreatedByUserID", Test.CreatedByUserID);

                object result = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
                if (result != null && int.TryParse(result.ToString(), out int insertedID))
                    TestID = insertedID;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }
            return TestID;
        }
    
    
   }

namespace DVLD_DataAccess
{
    public class clsTestTypeDataAccess {
        
        public static DataTable GetAllTestTypes() {
            DataTable dt = new DataTable();
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = @"SELECT TestTypeID, TestTypeTitle, TestTypeDescription, TestTypeFees
                             FROM TestTypes";

            try {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);

                using SqlDataReader reader = command.ExecuteReader();

                if (reader.HasRows)
                    dt.Load(reader);

            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }

            return dt;
        }
        
        public static bool UpdateTestType(TestTypeDTO TestType) {

            int RowsAffected = 0;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = @"UPDATE TestTypes
                             SET TestTypeTitle = @TestTypeTitle, TestTypeDescription = @TestTypeDescription, TestTypeFees = @TestTypeFees
                             WHERE TestTypeID = @TestTypeID";

            try {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@TestTypeTitle", TestType.TestTypeTitle);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@TestTypeDescription", TestType.TestTypeDescription);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@TestTypeFees", TestType.TestTypeFees);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@TestTypeID", TestType.TestTypeID);

                RowsAffected = command.ExecuteNonQuery();

            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }

            return (RowsAffected > 0);

        }
        
        public static TestTypeDTO? GetTestTypeInfoByID(int TestTypeID) {
            
            TestTypeDTO TestType = null;
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = @"SELECT TestTypeID, TestTypeTitle, TestTypeDescription, TestTypeFees
                             FROM TestTypes
                             WHERE TestTypeID = @TestTypeID";
            try {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@TestTypeID", TestTypeID);

                using SqlDataReader reader = command.ExecuteReader();

                if (reader.Read())
                    TestType = new TestTypeDTO(reader.GetInt32(reader.GetOrdinal("TestTypeID")), reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("TestTypeTitle")), reader.GetString(reader.GetOrdinal("TestTypeDescription")), reader.GetDecimal(reader.GetOrdinal("TestTypeFees")));

            }
            catch(SqlException ex)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
            }

            return TestType;


        }

    }

﻿using DVLD_DTOs;
using System;

namespace DVLD_DataAccess

    public class clsUserDataAccess {
        public static int AddNewUser(UserDTO user) {
            int UserID = -1;

            using SqlConnection connection = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = @"INSERT INTO Users (PersonID, UserName, Password, IsActive)
                         VALUES (@PersonID, @UserName, @Password, @IsActive);
                         SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY();";


            try {
                connection.Open();

                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, connection);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@PersonID", user.PersonID);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@UserName", user.Username);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Password", user.Password);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@IsActive", user.IsActive);

                object result = command.ExecuteScalar();

                 if (result != null && int.TryParse(result.ToString(), out int insertedID))
                      UserID = insertedID;
                    }
                    catch (Exception ex)
                    {
                        clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(ex);
                    }
            return UserID;

        }

        public static UserDTO? GetUserInfoByUserID(int userID)
        {
            UserDTO? user = null;

            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = "SELECT * FROM Users WHERE UserID = @UserID";


            try { 
                con.Open();

                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@UserID", userID);

                using SqlDataReader reader = command.ExecuteReader();

                if (reader.Read()) 
                    user = new UserDTO((int)reader["UserID"], (int)reader["PersonID"], (string)reader["Username"],  (string)reader["Password"], (bool)reader["IsActive"]);
                        
            }
            catch(SqlException e)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(e);
            }

            return user; 
        }

        public static UserDTO? GetUserInfoByPersonID(int PersonID)
        {
            UserDTO? user = null;

            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = "SELECT * FROM Users WHERE PersonID = @PersonID";


            try
            {
                con.Open();

                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@PersonID", PersonID);

                using SqlDataReader reader = command.ExecuteReader();

                if (reader.Read())
                    user = new UserDTO((int)reader["UserID"], (int)reader["PersonID"], (string)reader["Username"], (string)reader["Password"], (bool)reader["IsActive"]);

            }
            catch (SqlException e)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(e);
            }

            return user;
        }

        public static UserDTO? GetUserInfoByUsernameAndPassword(string Username, string Password) {
            UserDTO? user = null;

            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = @"SELECT * 
                             FROM users
                             WHERE Username = @Username AND Password = @Password";

            try
            {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Username", Username);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Password", Password);

                using SqlDataReader reader = command.ExecuteReader();
                
                if(reader.Read())
                    user = new UserDTO((int)reader["UserID"], (int)reader["PersonID"], (string)reader["Username"], (string)reader["Password"], (bool)reader["IsActive"]);

            }
            catch (SqlException e) {
                Console.WriteLine(e);
            }

            return user;
        }

        public static DataTable GetAllUsers() {
            DataTable dt = new DataTable();
            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString); ;
            string query = @"SELECT Users.UserID, Users.PersonID, (People.FirstName + ' ' + People.SecondName + ' ' + People.ThirdName + ' ' + People.LastName) as FullName, Users.UserName,  Users.IsActive
                             FROM Users
                             INNER JOIN People ON Users.PersonID = People.PersonID";

            try
            {
                con.Open();

                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);

                SqlDataReader reader = command.ExecuteReader();

                if(reader.HasRows)
                    dt.Load(reader);


            }
            catch (SqlException e)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(e);
            }

            return dt;

        }

        public static bool UpdateUser(UserDTO user) {
            int RowsAffected = 0;

            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);

            string query = @"UPDATE users
                     SET Username = @Username, 
                         Password = @Password,
                         IsActive = @IsActive
                     WHERE UserID = @UserID";

            try
            {
                con.Open();
                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);

                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Username", user.Username);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@Password", user.Password);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@IsActive", user.IsActive);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@UserID", user.UserID);

                RowsAffected = command.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }
            catch (SqlException e)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(e);
            }

            return (RowsAffected > 0);
        }

        public static bool IsUserExist(int userID) {
            bool isFound = false;

            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = "SELECT Found=1 FROM Users WHERE UserID = @UserID";

            try
            {
                con.Open();

                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@UserID", userID);

                object result = command.ExecuteScalar();

                if (result != null)
                    isFound = true;
            }
            catch (SqlException e)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(e);
            }

            return isFound;
        }

        public static bool DeleteUser(int userID)
        {
            int rowsAffected = 0;

            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = "DELETE FROM Users WHERE UserID = @UserID";

            try
            {
                con.Open();

                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@UserID", userID);

                rowsAffected = command.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }
            catch (SqlException e)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(e);
            }

            return (rowsAffected > 0);
        }

        public static bool ChangePassword(int UserID, string OldPassword, string NewPassword) {
            int rowsAffected = 0;

            using SqlConnection con = new SqlConnection(clsDataAccessSettings.ConnectionString);
            string query = @"UPDATE Users 
                             SET Password = @NewPassword 
                             WHERE UserID = @UserID AND Password = @OldPassword";

            try
            {
                con.Open();

                using SqlCommand command = new SqlCommand(query, con);
                
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@UserID", UserID);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@OldPassword", OldPassword);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@NewPassword", NewPassword);

                rowsAffected = command.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }
            catch (SqlException e)
            {
                clsDataAccessSettings.LogError(e);
            }

            return (rowsAffected > 0);
        }
    }

    public class UserDTO {
        public int UserID;
        public int PersonID;
        public string Username;
        public string Password;
        public bool IsActive;

        public UserDTO()
        {
            this.UserID = -1;
            this.PersonID = -1;
            this.Username = string.Empty;
            this.Password = string.Empty;
            this.IsActive = false;
        }

        public UserDTO(int UserID, int PersonID, string username, string password, bool IsActive)
        {
            this.UserID = UserID;
            this.PersonID = PersonID;
            this.Username = username;
            this.Password = password;
            this.IsActive = IsActive;

namespace DVLD_Presentation {
    public static class clsFormatHelper {

        public static System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath GetRoundedRectanglePath(Rectangle rect, int radius = 10)
        {
            System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = new System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath();
            path.StartFigure();
            path.AddArc(rect.X, rect.Y, radius, radius, 180, 90);
            path.AddArc(rect.Right - radius, rect.Y, radius, radius, 270, 90);
            path.AddArc(rect.Right - radius, rect.Bottom - radius, radius, radius, 0, 90);
            path.AddArc(rect.X, rect.Bottom - radius, radius, radius, 90, 90);
            path.CloseFigure();
            return path;
        }

        public static void StyleDataGridView(DataGridView dgv)
        {

            dgv.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None;
            dgv.BackgroundColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            dgv.GridColor = Color.Silver;
            dgv.CellBorderStyle = DataGridViewCellBorderStyle.SingleHorizontal;
            dgv.RowHeadersVisible = false;
            dgv.SelectionMode = DataGridViewSelectionMode.FullRowSelect;
            dgv.AllowUserToAddRows = false;
            dgv.AutoSizeColumnsMode = DataGridViewAutoSizeColumnsMode.Fill;

            dgv.DefaultCellStyle.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            dgv.DefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = Color.White;
            dgv.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionBackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316");
            dgv.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionForeColor = Color.White;
            dgv.RowTemplate.Height = 40;

            dgv.EnableHeadersVisualStyles = false;
            dgv.ColumnHeadersBorderStyle = DataGridViewHeaderBorderStyle.None;
            dgv.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.BackColor = Color.White;
            dgv.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = Color.Black;

            dgv.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.Font = new Font("Nunito", 11F, FontStyle.Bold);
            dgv.ColumnHeadersHeight = 45;
        }

        public static void SunnyStyleButton(UIButton btn, bool isPrimary = true)
        {
            if (btn == null)
                return;

            btn.Style = UIStyle.Custom;

            btn.Font = new Font("Nunito", 11F, FontStyle.Bold);
            btn.Radius = 10;

            if (isPrimary)
            {
                btn.FillColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#F97316");       // لون الخلفية
                btn.FillHoverColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#FB8C3D");  // عند التمرير
                btn.FillPressColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#EA580C");  // عند الضغط
                btn.RectColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#F97316");       // لون الإطار
                btn.RectHoverColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#FB8C3D");
                btn.RectPressColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#EA580C");
                btn.ForeColor = Color.White;                               // لون النص
            }
            else
            {
                btn.FillColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#111827");
                btn.FillHoverColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1F2937");
                btn.FillPressColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#374151");
                btn.RectColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#F97316");       // إطار برتقالي
                btn.RectHoverColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#FB8C3D");
                btn.RectPressColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#EA580C");
                btn.ForeColor = Color.White;
            }
        }

        public static void StyleButton(Button btn) {
            if (btn == null) 
                return;

            btn.FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat;
            btn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            btn.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316");
            btn.ForeColor = Color.White;
            btn.Font = new Font("Nunito", 11F, FontStyle.Bold);

            MakeButtonRounded(btn, 15);
        }

        public static void MakeButtonRounded(Button btn, int radius) {
            using (GraphicsPath path = new GraphicsPath()) {
                path.StartFigure();
                path.AddArc(0, 0, radius, radius, 180, 90);
                path.AddArc(btn.Width - radius, 0, radius, radius, 270, 90);
                path.AddArc(btn.Width - radius, btn.Height - radius, radius, radius, 0, 90);
                path.AddArc(0, btn.Height - radius, radius, radius, 90, 90);
                path.CloseFigure();
                btn.Region = new Region(path);
            }
        }

        public static void StyleDateTimePicker(DateTimePicker dtp)
        {
            dtp.Format = DateTimePickerFormat.Custom;
            dtp.CustomFormat = "yyyy-MM-dd";

            System.Reflection.MethodInfo mi = typeof(Control).GetMethod("SetStyle",
                System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Instance);

            if (mi != null)
            {
                mi.Invoke(dtp, new object[] { ControlStyles.UserPaint, true });
            }

            dtp.Paint += (s, e) =>
            {
                e.Graphics.Clear(Color.FromArgb(45, 54, 70));

                using (Brush textBrush = new SolidBrush(Color.White))
                {
                    e.Graphics.DrawString(dtp.Text, dtp.Font, textBrush, 4, 4);
                }

                Rectangle dropButtonRect = new Rectangle(dtp.Width - 20, 0, 20, dtp.Height);
                using (Brush bgBrush = new SolidBrush(Color.White))
                {
                    e.Graphics.FillRectangle(bgBrush, dropButtonRect);
                }

                int arrowX = dtp.Width - 13;
                int arrowY = dtp.Height / 2 - 2;
                Point[] arrow = new Point[]
                {
            new Point(arrowX, arrowY),
            new Point(arrowX + 6, arrowY),
            new Point(arrowX + 3, arrowY + 4)
                };
                using (Brush arrowBrush = new SolidBrush(Color.Black))
                {
                    e.Graphics.FillPolygon(arrowBrush, arrow);
                }
            };
        }

        public static void MakePictureBoxCircular(PictureBox pb)
        {
            if (pb == null)
                return;

            using (GraphicsPath path = new GraphicsPath())
            {
                path.AddEllipse(0, 0, pb.Width, pb.Height);
                pb.Region = new Region(path);
            }
        }

        public static void MakePictureBoxRoundedCorners(PictureBox pb, int radius)
        {
            if (pb == null)
                return;

            Rectangle rect = new Rectangle(0, 0, pb.Width, pb.Height);

            using (GraphicsPath path = GetRoundedRectanglePath(rect, radius))
            {
                pb.Region = new Region(path);
            }
        }

        public static void StyleContextMenu(ContextMenuStrip cms, Color? hoverColor = null) {
            if (cms == null) 
                return;

            Color selectedColor = hoverColor ?? Color.FromArgb(249, 115, 22);

            cms.Renderer = new ToolStripProfessionalRenderer(new CustomMenuColors(selectedColor));
            cms.BackColor = Color.FromArgb(12, 22, 50);
            cms.ForeColor = Color.White;
            cms.ShowImageMargin = false;
            cms.ShowCheckMargin = false;
        }

        public class CustomMenuColors : ProfessionalColorTable
        {
            private readonly Color _hoverColor;
            private readonly Color _darkBlue = Color.FromArgb(12, 22, 50);

            public CustomMenuColors(Color hoverColor)
            {
                _hoverColor = hoverColor;
            }

            public override Color MenuItemSelected => _hoverColor;
            public override Color MenuItemSelectedGradientBegin => _hoverColor;
            public override Color MenuItemSelectedGradientEnd => _hoverColor;
            public override Color MenuItemBorder => _hoverColor;
            public override Color MenuBorder => _hoverColor;

            public override Color ToolStripDropDownBackground => _darkBlue;
            public override Color ImageMarginGradientBegin => _darkBlue;
            public override Color ImageMarginGradientMiddle => _darkBlue;
            public override Color ImageMarginGradientEnd => _darkBlue;
        }

        public static void SetControlsColor(Panel pnl, Color textColor, Color iconColor)
        {
            if (pnl == null) return;

            foreach (Control ctrl in pnl.Controls)
            {
                if (ctrl is FontAwesome.Sharp.IconPictureBox icon)
                    icon.IconColor = iconColor;
                else if (ctrl is PictureBox pic)
                    pic.ForeColor = iconColor;
                else if (ctrl is Label lbl)
                    lbl.ForeColor = textColor;
            }
        }


    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

using System.Linq;

namespace DVLD_Presentation
{
    public partial class frmManageApplicationTypes : Form
    {
        private DataTable _dtShowApplicationTypes;
      
        public frmManageApplicationTypes()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void frmManageApplicationTypes_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            dgvShowApplicationTypes.BackColor = System.Drawing.ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            dgvShowApplicationTypes.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#374151");
            dgvShowApplicationTypes.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = Color.White;
            dgvShowApplicationTypes.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionBackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316");
            dgvShowApplicationTypes.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionForeColor = Color.White;

            clsFormatHelper.StyleDataGridView(dgvShowApplicationTypes);
            dgvShowApplicationTypes.AutoGenerateColumns = false;

            _RefreshApplicationTypes();
            
        }

        private void dgvShowApplicationTypes_CellDoubleClick(object sender, DataGridViewCellEventArgs e)
        {

            if (e.RowIndex < 0)
                return;

            int ApplicationTypeID = (int)dgvShowApplicationTypes.CurrentRow.Cells["ApplicationTypeID"].Value;

            frmUpdateApplicationType frm = new frmUpdateApplicationType(ApplicationTypeID);
            frm.ShowDialog();

            _RefreshApplicationTypes();
        }

        private void _RefreshApplicationTypes() {
            _dtShowApplicationTypes = clsApplicationTypeBusiness.GetAllApplicationTypes();
            dgvShowApplicationTypes.DataSource = _dtShowApplicationTypes;
            lblRecordsNumber.Text = _dtShowApplicationTypes.Rows.Count.ToString();
        }

    }
}

}﻿using DVLD_Business;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Application_Types
{
    public partial class frmUpdateApplicationType : Form
    {
        private int _ApplicationTypeID = -1;
        private clsApplicationTypeBusiness? _ApplicationType;

        public frmUpdateApplicationType(int ApplicationTypeID)
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _ApplicationTypeID = ApplicationTypeID;
        }

        private void _LoadData()
        {

            _ApplicationType = clsApplicationTypeBusiness.GetApplicationTypeInfoByID(_ApplicationTypeID);

            if (_ApplicationType == null) {
                MessageBox.Show("Application Type not found.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                this.Close();
                return;
            }

            lblAnswerID.Text = Convert.ToString(_ApplicationType.ApplicationTypeID);
            txtTitle.Text = _ApplicationType.ApplicationTypeTitle;
            txtFees.Text = Convert.ToString(_ApplicationType.ApplicationFees);


        }

        private void frmUpdateApplicationType_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _LoadData();
        }

        private void btnSave_Click(object sender, EventArgs e) {

            if(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtTitle.Text)) {
                MessageBox.Show("Title cannot be empty.", "Validation Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                txtTitle.Focus();
                return;
            }

            if (!decimal.TryParse(txtFees.Text.Trim(), out decimal Fees) || Fees < 0) {
                MessageBox.Show("Please enter a valid positive number for fees.", "Validation Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                txtFees.Focus();
                return;
            }

            _ApplicationType.ApplicationTypeTitle = txtTitle.Text.Trim();
            _ApplicationType.ApplicationFees = Fees;

            if(_ApplicationType.UpdateApplicationType()) {
                MessageBox.Show("Data Updated Successfully.", "Saved", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                this.Close();
            } 
            else
                MessageBox.Show("Error: Data was not updated.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);

        }
    }
}

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Licenses
{
    public partial class frmDetainLicense : Form
    {
        private clsLicenseBusiness? _License;
        private readonly int _LicenseID;

        public frmDetainLicense()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _LicenseID = -1;
        }

        public frmDetainLicense(int LicenseID)
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _LicenseID = LicenseID;
        }

        private void frmDetainLicense_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnSearch);
            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnDetain);

            if (_LicenseID != -1)
                _SearchForLicense(_LicenseID);

            if (_LicenseID != -1)
            {
                txtSearchLicense.Visible = false;

                pnlTextBorder.Visible = false;
                btnSearch.Visible = false;

                _SearchForLicense(_LicenseID);
            }

        }

        private void btnSearch_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtSearchLicense.Text))
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please enter a License ID to search.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            if (!int.TryParse(txtSearchLicense.Text.Trim(), out int LicenseID))
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please enter a valid numeric License ID.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            _SearchForLicense(LicenseID);
        }

        private void _SearchForLicense(int LicenseID)
        {
            txtSearchLicense.Text = LicenseID.ToString();

            ucDriverLicenseInfo.LoadInfo(LicenseID);
            _License = null;

            if (ucDriverLicenseInfo.SelectedLicenseInfo == null)
                return;

            clsLicenseBusiness License = ucDriverLicenseInfo.SelectedLicenseInfo;

            if (!_IsEligibleForDetain(License, out string ErrorMessage))
            {
                MessageBox.Show(ErrorMessage, "Not Eligible", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            _License = License;
            ucDetainInfo.LoadData();
        }

        private bool _IsEligibleForDetain(clsLicenseBusiness License, out string ErrorMessage)
        {
            ErrorMessage = "";

            if (!License.IsActive)
            {
                ErrorMessage = "This license is not active and cannot be detained.";
                return false;
            }

            if (clsDetainedLicenseBusiness.GetActiveDetainInfoByLicenseID(License.LicenseID) != null)
            {
                ErrorMessage = "This license is already detained and has not been released yet.";
                return false;
            }

            return true;
        }

        private void btnDetain_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_License == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please search for an eligible license first.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            if (!_IsEligibleForDetain(_License, out string ErrorMessage))
            {
                MessageBox.Show(ErrorMessage, "Not Eligible", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            decimal FineFees = ucDetainInfo.FineFees;

            if (FineFees < 0)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Fine Fees cannot be negative.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            clsDetainedLicenseBusiness NewDetain = new clsDetainedLicenseBusiness();
            NewDetain.LicenseID = _License.LicenseID;
            NewDetain.DetainDate = DateTime.Now;
            NewDetain.FineFees = FineFees;
            NewDetain.CreatedByUserID = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.UserID;
            NewDetain.IsReleased = false;

            if (!NewDetain.Save())
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Failed to detain the license.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            ucDetainInfo.SetDetainResult(NewDetain.DetainID, NewDetain.DetainDate);
            ucDriverLicenseInfo.LoadInfo(_License.LicenseID);

            MessageBox.Show("License detained successfully.", "Success", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
        }
    }

        private void pnlTextBorder_Paint(object sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Panel pnl = (Panel)sender;
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;

            using (Pen pen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316"), 2))
            {
                Rectangle rect = new Rectangle(0, 0, pnl.Width - 1, pnl.Height - 1);
                using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = clsFormatHelper.GetRoundedRectanglePath(rect, 15))
                {
                    using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937")))
                    {
                        e.Graphics.FillPath(brush, path);
                    }
                    e.Graphics.DrawPath(pen, path);
                }
            }
        }

        private void txtSearchLicense_TextChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            string SelectedFilter = cmbFilterBy.SelectedItem.ToString();
            string FilterColumn = "";

            switch (SelectedFilter)
            {
                case "Detain ID":
                    FilterColumn = "DetainID";
                    break;
                case "Is Released":
                    FilterColumn = "IsReleased";
                    break;
                case "National No":
                    FilterColumn = "NationalNo";
                    break;
                case "Full Name":
                    FilterColumn = "FullName";
                    break;
                case "Release Application ID":
                    FilterColumn = "ReleaseApplicationID";
                    break;
                default:
                    FilterColumn = "";
                    break;
            }

            if (txtSearchLicense.Text.Trim() == "")
            {
                _dtAllDetainedLicenses.DefaultView.RowFilter = "";
                return;
            }

            if (FilterColumn == "")
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Invalid filter selected", "Error");
                return;
            }

            if (FilterColumn == "DetainID" || FilterColumn == "ReleaseApplicationID")
            {
                if (int.TryParse(txtSearchLicense.Text.Trim(), out int Result))
                    _dtAllDetainedLicenses.DefaultView.RowFilter = string.Format("[{0}] = {1}", FilterColumn, Result);
                else
                    _dtAllDetainedLicenses.DefaultView.RowFilter = "1=0";
            }
            else if (FilterColumn == "IsReleased")
            {
                string Input = txtSearchLicense.Text.Trim().ToUpper();

                if (Input == "Y" || Input == "YES" || Input == "TRUE" || Input == "1")
                    _dtAllDetainedLicenses.DefaultView.RowFilter = "IsReleased = 1";
                else if (Input == "N" || Input == "NO" || Input == "FALSE" || Input == "0")
                    _dtAllDetainedLicenses.DefaultView.RowFilter = "IsReleased = 0";
                else
                    _dtAllDetainedLicenses.DefaultView.RowFilter = "1=0";
            }
            else
                _dtAllDetainedLicenses.DefaultView.RowFilter = string.Format("[{0}] LIKE '{1}%'", FilterColumn, txtSearchLicense.Text.Trim());
        }

        private void btnDetain_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (dgvShowDetainedLicenses.CurrentRow == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please select a license from the list first.", "Selection Required", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            int LicenseID = (int)dgvShowDetainedLicenses.CurrentRow.Cells["LicenseID"].Value;

            frmDetainLicense frm = new frmDetainLicense(LicenseID);
            frm.ShowDialog();

            _RefreshDetainedLicensesList();
        }

        private void btnRelease_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (dgvShowDetainedLicenses.SelectedRows.Count > 0)
            {
                int LicenseID = Convert.ToInt32(dgvShowDetainedLicenses.SelectedRows[0].Cells["LicenseID"].Value);

                frmReleaseDetainedLicense frm = new frmReleaseDetainedLicense(LicenseID);
                frm.ShowDialog();
                _RefreshDetainedLicensesList();
            }
            else
                MessageBox.Show("Please select a record from the list first.", "Selection Required", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
        }

        private void _RefreshDetainedLicensesList()
        {
            _dtAllDetainedLicenses = clsDetainedLicenseBusiness.GetAllDetainedLicenses();
            dgvShowDetainedLicenses.DataSource = _dtAllDetainedLicenses;
        }

        private void cmbFilterBy_SelectedIndexChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            txtSearchLicense.Visible = (cmbFilterBy.SelectedIndex != 0);
            pnlTextBorder.Visible = (cmbFilterBy.SelectedIndex != 0);

            if (txtSearchLicense.Visible)
            {
                txtSearchLicense.Text = "";
                txtSearchLicense.Focus();
            }
        }

        private void dgvShowDetainedLicenses_CellFormatting(object sender, DataGridViewCellFormattingEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.RowIndex < 0 || e.Value == null)
                return;

            if (dgvShowDetainedLicenses.Columns[e.ColumnIndex].Name == "IsReleased")
                e.Value = (e.Value.ToString() == "True") ? "Yes" : "No";

        }

        private void toolStripMenuItemShowPersonDetails_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (dgvShowDetainedLicenses.CurrentRow == null)
                return;

            string NationalNo = (string)dgvShowDetainedLicenses.CurrentRow.Cells["NationalNo"].Value;
            clsPersonBusiness? PersonInfo = clsPersonBusiness.Find(NationalNo);

            if (PersonInfo != null)
            {
                frmShowPersonInfo frm = new frmShowPersonInfo(PersonInfo.PersonID);
                frm.ShowDialog();
            }
            else
                MessageBox.Show("Could not retrieve Person Information.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);

        }

        private void toolStripMenuItemShowLicenseDetails_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (dgvShowDetainedLicenses.CurrentRow == null)
                return;

            int LicenseID = (int)dgvShowDetainedLicenses.CurrentRow.Cells["LicenseID"].Value;

            frmShowDriverLicenseInfo frm = new frmShowDriverLicenseInfo(LicenseID);
            frm.ShowDialog();

        }

        private void toolStripMenuItemShowPersonLicenseHistory_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (dgvShowDetainedLicenses.CurrentRow == null)
                return;

            string NationalNo = (string)dgvShowDetainedLicenses.CurrentRow.Cells["NationalNo"].Value;
            clsPersonBusiness? PersonInfo = clsPersonBusiness.Find(NationalNo);

            if (PersonInfo != null)
            {
                frmShowPersonLicenseHistory frm = new frmShowPersonLicenseHistory(PersonInfo.PersonID);
                frm.ShowDialog();
            }
            else
                MessageBox.Show("Could not retrieve Person Information.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);

        }

        private void toolStripMenuItemReleaseDetainedLicense_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (dgvShowDetainedLicenses.CurrentRow == null)
                return;

            int LicenseID = (int)dgvShowDetainedLicenses.CurrentRow.Cells["LicenseID"].Value;

            frmReleaseDetainedLicense frm = new frmReleaseDetainedLicense(LicenseID);
            frm.ShowDialog();

            _RefreshDetainedLicensesList();

        }

        private void cxmManageDetainedLicenses_Opening(object sender, CancelEventArgs e) {

            if (dgvShowDetainedLicenses.CurrentRow == null)
                return;

            bool IsReleased = (bool)dgvShowDetainedLicenses.CurrentRow.Cells["IsReleased"].Value;

            toolStripMenuItemReleaseDetainedLicense.Enabled = !IsReleased;

        }
    }
}

using DVLD_Presentation.User_Controls;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Detain_License
{
    public partial class frmReleaseDetainedLicense : Form
    {
        private clsLicenseBusiness? _License;
        private readonly int _LicenseID;

        public frmReleaseDetainedLicense()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _LicenseID = -1;
        }

        public frmReleaseDetainedLicense(int LicenseID) : this()
        {
            _LicenseID = LicenseID;
        }

        private void frmReleaseDetainedLicense_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnSearch);
            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnRelease);

            if (_LicenseID != -1)
                _SearchForLicense(_LicenseID);
        }

        private void btnSearch_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtSearchLicense.Text))
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please enter a License ID to search.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            if (!int.TryParse(txtSearchLicense.Text.Trim(), out int LicenseID))
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please enter a valid numeric License ID.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            _SearchForLicense(LicenseID);
        }

        private void _SearchForLicense(int LicenseID)
        {
            txtSearchLicense.Text = LicenseID.ToString();

            ucDriverLicenseInfo.LoadInfo(LicenseID);
            _License = null;

            if (ucDriverLicenseInfo.SelectedLicenseInfo == null)
                return;

            clsLicenseBusiness License = ucDriverLicenseInfo.SelectedLicenseInfo;

            if (!_IsEligibleForRelease(License, out string ErrorMessage))
            {
                MessageBox.Show(ErrorMessage, "Not Eligible", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            _License = License;
            ucReleaseInfo.LoadData(_License.LicenseID);
        }

        private bool _IsEligibleForRelease(clsLicenseBusiness License, out string ErrorMessage)
        {
            ErrorMessage = "";

            if (clsDetainedLicenseBusiness.GetActiveDetainInfoByLicenseID(License.LicenseID) == null)
            {
                ErrorMessage = "This license is not currently detained.";
                return false;
            }

            return true;
        }

        private void btnRelease_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_License == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please search for a detained license first.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            if (!_IsEligibleForRelease(_License, out string ErrorMessage))
            {
                MessageBox.Show(ErrorMessage, "Not Eligible", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            clsDetainedLicenseBusiness? Detain = ucReleaseInfo.SelectedDetainInfo;

            if (Detain == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Could not load the detain information for this license.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            clsApplicationTypeBusiness? ApplicationType = clsApplicationTypeBusiness.GetApplicationTypeInfoByID(5);
            decimal ApplicationFees = (ApplicationType != null) ? ApplicationType.ApplicationFees : 0;

            clsApplicationBusiness ReleaseApplication = new clsApplicationBusiness();
            ReleaseApplication.ApplicantPersonID = _License.DriverInfo.PersonInfo.PersonID;
            ReleaseApplication.ApplicationDate = DateTime.Now;
            ReleaseApplication.ApplicationTypeID = 5;
            ReleaseApplication.PaidFees = ApplicationFees;
            ReleaseApplication.CreatedByUserID = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.UserID;

            if (!ReleaseApplication.Save())
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Failed to create the release application.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            if (!Detain.Release(clsGlobal.CurrentUser.UserID, ReleaseApplication.ApplicationID))
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Failed to release the license.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            ReleaseApplication.Completed();

            ucReleaseInfo.SetReleaseResult(ReleaseApplication.ApplicationID);
            ucDriverLicenseInfo.LoadInfo(_License.LicenseID);

            MessageBox.Show("License released successfully.", "Success", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
        }
    }

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Drivers
