    public partial class frmManageDrivers : Form
    {

        private static DataTable? _dtAllDrivers;

        public frmManageDrivers()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void frmManageDrivers_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            dgvShowDrivers.AutoGenerateColumns = false;
            _RefreshDriversList();

            txtSearchDriver.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None;
            txtSearchDriver.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            txtSearchDriver.ForeColor = Color.White;
            txtSearchDriver.PlaceholderText = "Search...";
            txtSearchDriver.TextAlign = HorizontalAlignment.Left;
            txtSearchDriver.Top = (pnlTextBorder.Height - txtSearchDriver.Height) / 2;
            txtSearchDriver.Left = 12;
            txtSearchDriver.Width = pnlTextBorder.Width - 24;

            cmbFilterBy.FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat;
            cmbFilterBy.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            cmbFilterBy.ForeColor = Color.White;

            dgvShowDrivers.BackColor = System.Drawing.ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            dgvShowDrivers.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#374151");
            dgvShowDrivers.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = Color.White;
            dgvShowDrivers.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionBackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316");
            dgvShowDrivers.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionForeColor = Color.White;

            clsFormatHelper.StyleDataGridView(dgvShowDrivers);

            dgvShowDrivers.Columns["DriverID"].FillWeight = 12;
            dgvShowDrivers.Columns["PersonID"].FillWeight = 12;
            dgvShowDrivers.Columns["NationalNo"].FillWeight = 14;
            dgvShowDrivers.Columns["FullName"].FillWeight = 34;
            dgvShowDrivers.Columns["Date"].FillWeight = 14;
            dgvShowDrivers.Columns["ActiveLicenses"].FillWeight = 14;

            if (cmbFilterBy.Items.Count > 0)
                cmbFilterBy.SelectedIndex = 0;

        }

        private void _RefreshDriversList()
        {
            _dtAllDrivers = clsDriverBusiness.GetAllDrivers();
            dgvShowDrivers.DataSource = _dtAllDrivers;
        }


        private void cmbFilterBy_SelectedIndexChanged(object sender, EventArgs e) {
            
            bool IsNone = (cmbFilterBy.Text == "None" || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cmbFilterBy.Text));

            txtSearchDriver.Visible = !IsNone;
            pnlTextBorder.Visible = !IsNone;

            if (txtSearchDriver.Visible)
            {
                txtSearchDriver.Text = "";
                txtSearchDriver.Focus();
            }
            else
                if (_dtAllDrivers != null)
                    _dtAllDrivers.DefaultView.RowFilter = "";

        }

        private void txtSearchDriver_TextChanged(object sender, EventArgs e) {

            if (cmbFilterBy.SelectedItem == null)
                return;

            string SelectedFilter = cmbFilterBy.SelectedItem.ToString();
            string FilterColumn = "";

            switch (SelectedFilter)
            {
                case "Driver ID":
                    FilterColumn = "DriverID";
                    break;
                case "Person ID":
                    FilterColumn = "PersonID";
                    break;
                case "National No.":
                    FilterColumn = "NationalNo";
                    break;
                case "Full Name":
                    FilterColumn = "FullName";
                    break;
                default:
                    FilterColumn = "";
                    break;

            }
        }

        private void pnlTextBorder_Paint(object sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Panel pnl = (Panel)sender;
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;

            using (Pen pen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316"), 2))
            {
                Rectangle rect = new Rectangle(0, 0, pnl.Width - 1, pnl.Height - 1);
                using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = clsFormatHelper.GetRoundedRectanglePath(rect, 15))
                {
                    using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937")))
                    {
                        e.Graphics.FillPath(brush, path);
                    }
                    e.Graphics.DrawPath(pen, path);
                }
            }
        }

    }
}

namespace DVLD_Presentation.International_Driving_License
{
    public partial class frmAddInternationalLicenseApplication : Form
    {
        private clsInternationalLicenseBusiness _PendingInternationalLicense;

        public frmAddInternationalLicenseApplication()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void frmAddInternationalLicenseApplication_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnSearch);
            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnAdd);

            txtSearchLicense.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None;
            txtSearchLicense.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            txtSearchLicense.ForeColor = Color.White;
            txtSearchLicense.PlaceholderText = "Search Employees...";

            txtSearchLicense.TextAlign = HorizontalAlignment.Left;
            txtSearchLicense.Top = (pnlTextBorder.Height - txtSearchLicense.Height) / 2;
            txtSearchLicense.Left = 12;
            txtSearchLicense.Width = pnlTextBorder.Width - 24;

        }

        private void btnSearch_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtSearchLicense.Text))
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please enter a License ID to search.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            if (!int.TryParse(txtSearchLicense.Text.Trim(), out int LicenseID))
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please enter a valid numeric License ID.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            ucDriverLicenseInfo.LoadInfo(LicenseID);
            _PendingInternationalLicense = null;

            if (ucDriverLicenseInfo.SelectedLicenseInfo == null)
                return;

            clsLicenseBusiness License = ucDriverLicenseInfo.SelectedLicenseInfo;

            if (!_IsEligible(License, out string ErrorMessage))
            {
                MessageBox.Show(ErrorMessage, "Not Eligible", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            _PendingInternationalLicense = _BuildPendingInternationalLicense(License);
            ucApplicationInfo.LoadApplicationInfo(_PendingInternationalLicense);
        }

        private bool _IsEligible(clsLicenseBusiness License, out string ErrorMessage)
        {
            ErrorMessage = "";

            if (License.LicenseClassInfo.LicenseClassID != 3)
            {
                ErrorMessage = "International licenses can only be issued for Class 3 (Regular) licenses.";
                return false;
            }

            if (!License.IsActive)
            {
                ErrorMessage = "This license is not active.";
                return false;
            }

            if (clsInternationalLicenseBusiness.GetActiveInternationalLicenseIDByDriverID(License.DriverID) != -1)
            {
                ErrorMessage = "This driver already has an active International License.";
                return false;
            }

            return true;
        }

        private clsInternationalLicenseBusiness _BuildPendingInternationalLicense(clsLicenseBusiness License)
        {
            clsInternationalLicenseBusiness? InternationalLicense = new clsInternationalLicenseBusiness();

            InternationalLicense.DriverID = License.DriverID;
            InternationalLicense.IssuedUsingLocalLicenseID = License.LicenseID;
            InternationalLicense.IssueDate = DateTime.Now;
            InternationalLicense.CreatedByUserID = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.UserID;

            InternationalLicense.ApplicationInfo.ApplicantPersonID = License.DriverInfo.PersonInfo.PersonID;
            InternationalLicense.ApplicationInfo.ApplicationDate = DateTime.Now;

            clsApplicationTypeBusiness ApplicationType = clsApplicationTypeBusiness.GetApplicationTypeInfoByID(InternationalLicense.ApplicationInfo.ApplicationTypeID);

            InternationalLicense.ApplicationInfo.PaidFees = (ApplicationType != null) ? ApplicationType.ApplicationFees : 0;

            return InternationalLicense;
        }

        private void btnAdd_Click(object sender, EventArgs e) {

            if (_PendingInternationalLicense == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please search for an eligible license first.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            if (_PendingInternationalLicense.Save())
            {
                MessageBox.Show("International License issued successfully.", "Success", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                ucApplicationInfo.LoadApplicationInfo(_PendingInternationalLicense);
            }
            else
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Failed to issue the International License.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            }
        }

        private void lnkShowLicenseInfo_LinkClicked(object sender, LinkLabelLinkClickedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (_PendingInternationalLicense == null || _PendingInternationalLicense.InternationalLicenseID == -1)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("This International License has not been issued yet.", "Not Available", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            frmShowDriverInternationalLicenseInfo frm = new frmShowDriverInternationalLicenseInfo(_PendingInternationalLicense.InternationalLicenseID);
            frm.ShowDialog();
        }

        private void lnkShowLicensesHistory_LinkClicked(object sender, LinkLabelLinkClickedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (ucDriverLicenseInfo.SelectedLicenseInfo == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please search for a license first.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            int PersonID = ucDriverLicenseInfo.SelectedLicenseInfo.DriverInfo.PersonInfo.PersonID;

            frmShowPersonLicenseHistory frm = new frmShowPersonLicenseHistory(PersonID);
            frm.ShowDialog();
        }


        private void pnlTextBorder_Paint(object sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Panel pnl = (Panel)sender;
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;

            using (Pen pen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316"), 2))
            {
                Rectangle rect = new Rectangle(0, 0, pnl.Width - 1, pnl.Height - 1);
                using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = clsFormatHelper.GetRoundedRectanglePath(rect, 15))
                {

                    using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937")))
                    {
                        e.Graphics.FillPath(brush, path);
                    }
                    e.Graphics.DrawPath(pen, path);
                }
            }
        }

        private void txtSearchLicense_TextChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }
    }

            clsFormatHelper.StyleDataGridView(dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory);

            if (cmbFilterBy.Items.Count > 0)
                cmbFilterBy.SelectedIndex = 0;

            dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.AutoGenerateColumns = false;

            dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.Columns["InternationalLicenseID"].FillWeight = 15;
            dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.Columns["ApplicationIDInternatonal"].FillWeight = 15;
            dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.Columns["LocalLicenseID"].FillWeight = 15;
            dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.Columns["IssueDateInternational"].FillWeight = 20;
            dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.Columns["ExpirationDateInternational"].FillWeight = 20;
            dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.Columns["IsActiveInternational"].FillWeight = 15;

            _RefreshInternationalLicenseApplications();
        }

        private void _RefreshInternationalLicenseApplications()
        {
            _dtInternationalLicenseApplications = clsInternationalLicenseBusiness.GetAllInternationalLicenses();
            dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.DataSource = _dtInternationalLicenseApplications;
        }

        private void btnAddInternationalDrivingLicenseApplication_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            frmAddInternationalLicenseApplication frm = new frmAddInternationalLicenseApplication();
            frm.ShowDialog();

            _RefreshInternationalLicenseApplications();
        }

        private void txtSearchInternationalDrivingLicenseApplication_TextChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (cmbFilterBy.SelectedItem == null)
                return;

            string SelectedFilter = cmbFilterBy.SelectedItem.ToString();
            string FilterColumn = "";

            switch (SelectedFilter)
            {
                case "Int.License ID":
                    FilterColumn = "InternationalLicenseID";
                    break;
                case "Application ID":
                case "L.D.L.AppID": // Kept fallback just in case you haven't renamed the combobox items yet
                    FilterColumn = "ApplicationID";
                    break;
                case "Local License ID":
                    FilterColumn = "LocalLicenseID";
                    break;
                case "Status":
                case "Is Active":
                    FilterColumn = "IsActive";
                    break;
                default:
                    FilterColumn = "";
                    break;
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtSearchInternationalDrivingLicenseApplication.Text) || FilterColumn == "")
            {
                _dtInternationalLicenseApplications.DefaultView.RowFilter = "";
                return;
            }

            if (FilterColumn == "InternationalLicenseID" || FilterColumn == "ApplicationID" || FilterColumn == "LocalLicenseID")
            {
                if (int.TryParse(txtSearchInternationalDrivingLicenseApplication.Text.Trim(), out int Result))
                    _dtInternationalLicenseApplications.DefaultView.RowFilter = string.Format("[{0}] = {1}", FilterColumn, Result);
                else
                    _dtInternationalLicenseApplications.DefaultView.RowFilter = "1=0"; // Prevent crash on invalid string entry
            }
            else if (FilterColumn == "IsActive")
            {
                string searchVal = txtSearchInternationalDrivingLicenseApplication.Text.Trim().ToLower();
                if (searchVal.StartsWith("y") || searchVal.StartsWith("1") || searchVal.StartsWith("t"))
                    _dtInternationalLicenseApplications.DefaultView.RowFilter = string.Format("[{0}] = true", FilterColumn);
                else if (searchVal.StartsWith("n") || searchVal.StartsWith("0") || searchVal.StartsWith("f"))
                    _dtInternationalLicenseApplications.DefaultView.RowFilter = string.Format("[{0}] = false", FilterColumn);
            }
            else
            {
                _dtInternationalLicenseApplications.DefaultView.RowFilter = string.Format("[{0}] LIKE '{1}%'", FilterColumn, txtSearchInternationalDrivingLicenseApplication.Text.Trim());
            }
        }

        private void dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory_CellFormatting(object sender, DataGridViewCellFormattingEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.RowIndex < 0 || e.Value == null)
                return;

            if (dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.Columns[e.ColumnIndex].Name == "IsActiveInternational")
            {
                if (bool.TryParse(e.Value.ToString(), out bool isActive))
                {
                    e.Value = isActive ? "Yes" : "No";
                    e.FormattingApplied = true;
                }
            }
        }

        private void pnlTextBorder_Paint(object sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Panel pnl = (Panel)sender;
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;

            using (Pen pen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316"), 2))
            {
                Rectangle rect = new Rectangle(0, 0, pnl.Width - 1, pnl.Height - 1);
                using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = clsFormatHelper.GetRoundedRectanglePath(rect, 15))
                {
                    using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937")))
                    {
                        e.Graphics.FillPath(brush, path);
                    }
                    e.Graphics.DrawPath(pen, path);
                }
            }
        }

        private void ShowPersonDetailsToolStripMenuItem_Click(object sender, EventArgs e) {
            if (dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.CurrentRow == null)
                return;

            int ApplicationID = (int)dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.CurrentRow.Cells["ApplicationIDInternatonal"].Value;

            clsApplicationBusiness? ApplicationInfo = clsApplicationBusiness.GetApplicationInfoByID(ApplicationID);

            if (ApplicationInfo != null)
            {
                frmShowPersonInfo frm = new frmShowPersonInfo(ApplicationInfo.ApplicantPersonID);
                frm.ShowDialog();
            }
            else
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Could not retrieve Person Information.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            }
        }

        private void ShowLicenseDetailsToolStripMenuItem_Click(object sender, EventArgs e) {

            if (dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.CurrentRow == null)
                return;

            int InternationalLicenseID = (int)dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.CurrentRow.Cells["InternationalLicenseID"].Value;

            frmShowDriverInternationalLicenseInfo frm = new frmShowDriverInternationalLicenseInfo(InternationalLicenseID);
            frm.ShowDialog();

        }

        private void ShowPersonLicenseHistoryToolStripMenuItem_Click(object sender, EventArgs e) {

            if (dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.CurrentRow == null)
                return;

            int ApplicationID = (int)dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.CurrentRow.Cells["ApplicationIDInternatonal"].Value;

            clsApplicationBusiness? ApplicationInfo = clsApplicationBusiness.GetApplicationInfoByID(ApplicationID);

            if (ApplicationInfo != null) {
                frmShowPersonLicenseHistory frm = new frmShowPersonLicenseHistory(ApplicationInfo.ApplicantPersonID);
                frm.ShowDialog();
            }
            else
                MessageBox.Show("Could not retrieve Person Information.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);

        }

    }

}﻿using DVLD_Business;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Licenses
{
    public partial class frmLicenseReplacement : Form
    {

        private clsLicenseBusiness? _OldLicense;

        public frmLicenseReplacement()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void frmLicenseReplacement_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnSearch);
            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnIssueReplacement);
        }

        private void _UpdateTitle()
        {
            lblTitle.Text = rbDamagedLicense.Checked ? "Replacement for Damaged License" : "Replacement for Lost License";
        }

        private void rbDamagedLicense_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _UpdateTitle();
        }

        private void rbLostLicense_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _UpdateTitle();
        }

        private int _GetSelectedApplicationTypeID()
        {
            return rbDamagedLicense.Checked ? 4 : 3;
        }

        private byte _GetSelectedIssueReason() {
            return rbDamagedLicense.Checked ? (byte)4 : (byte)3;
        }

        private void btnSearch_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtSearchLicense.Text))
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please enter a License ID to search.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            if (!int.TryParse(txtSearchLicense.Text.Trim(), out int LicenseID))
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please enter a valid numeric License ID.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            ucDriverLicenseInfo.LoadInfo(LicenseID);
            _OldLicense = null;

            if (ucDriverLicenseInfo.SelectedLicenseInfo == null)
                return;

            clsLicenseBusiness License = ucDriverLicenseInfo.SelectedLicenseInfo;

            if (!_IsEligibleForReplacement(License, out string ErrorMessage))
            {
                MessageBox.Show(ErrorMessage, "Not Eligible", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            _OldLicense = License;
            ucApplicationInfoForLicenseReplacement.LoadData(_OldLicense.LicenseID, _GetSelectedApplicationTypeID());

        }

        private bool _IsEligibleForReplacement(clsLicenseBusiness License, out string ErrorMessage)
        {

            ErrorMessage = "";

            if (!License.IsActive)
            {
                ErrorMessage = "This license is not active and cannot be replaced.";
                return false;
            }

            if (License.ExpirationDate.Date < DateTime.Now.Date)
            {
                ErrorMessage = "This license has expired. Only non-expired licenses can be replaced.";
                return false;
            }

            return true;
        }

        private void pnlTextBorder_Paint(object sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Panel pnl = (Panel)sender;
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;

            using (Pen pen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316"), 2))
            {
                Rectangle rect = new Rectangle(0, 0, pnl.Width - 1, pnl.Height - 1);
                using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = clsFormatHelper.GetRoundedRectanglePath(rect, 15))
                {
                    using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937")))
                    {
                        e.Graphics.FillPath(brush, path);
                    }
                    e.Graphics.DrawPath(pen, path);
                }
            }
        }

        private void btnIssueReplacement_Click(object sender, EventArgs e) {

            if (_OldLicense == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please search for an eligible license first.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            // Re-validate in case the state changed since the search.
            if (!_IsEligibleForReplacement(_OldLicense, out string ErrorMessage))
            {
                MessageBox.Show(ErrorMessage, "Not Eligible", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            if (_OldLicense.LicenseClassInfo == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Could not load the license class information for this license.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            if (_OldLicense.DriverInfo == null || _OldLicense.DriverInfo.PersonInfo == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Could not load the driver/person information for this license.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            int ApplicationTypeID = _GetSelectedApplicationTypeID();
            byte IssueReason = _GetSelectedIssueReason();

            clsApplicationTypeBusiness ApplicationType = clsApplicationTypeBusiness.GetApplicationTypeInfoByID(ApplicationTypeID);
            decimal ApplicationFees = (ApplicationType != null) ? ApplicationType.ApplicationFees : 0;

            clsApplicationBusiness ReplacementApplication = new clsApplicationBusiness();
            ReplacementApplication.ApplicantPersonID = _OldLicense.DriverInfo.PersonInfo.PersonID;
            ReplacementApplication.ApplicationDate = DateTime.Now;
            ReplacementApplication.ApplicationTypeID = ApplicationTypeID;
            ReplacementApplication.PaidFees = ApplicationFees;
            ReplacementApplication.CreatedByUserID = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.UserID;

            if (!ReplacementApplication.Save())
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Failed to create the replacement application.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            clsLicenseBusiness NewLicense = new clsLicenseBusiness();
            NewLicense.ApplicationID = ReplacementApplication.ApplicationID;
            NewLicense.DriverID = _OldLicense.DriverID;
            NewLicense.LicenseClassID = _OldLicense.LicenseClassID;
            NewLicense.IssueDate = DateTime.Now;
            NewLicense.ExpirationDate = DateTime.Now.AddYears(_OldLicense.LicenseClassInfo.DefaultValidityLength);
            NewLicense.PaidFees = _OldLicense.LicenseClassInfo.ClassFees;
            NewLicense.IsActive = true;
            NewLicense.IssueReason = IssueReason;
            NewLicense.CreatedByUserID = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.UserID;

            string ReplacementTrace = rbDamagedLicense.Checked
                ? $"Replaced (Damaged) from License #{_OldLicense.LicenseID}."
                : $"Replaced (Lost) from License #{_OldLicense.LicenseID}.";
            NewLicense.Notes = ReplacementTrace;

            if (!NewLicense.Save())
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Failed to issue the replacement license.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            _OldLicense.IsActive = false;
            _OldLicense.Save();

            ReplacementApplication.Completed();

            ucApplicationInfoForLicenseReplacement.SetReplacementResult(ReplacementApplication.ApplicationID, NewLicense.LicenseID);
            ucDriverLicenseInfo.LoadInfo(_OldLicense.LicenseID);

            MessageBox.Show("Driving license replacement issued successfully.", "Success", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);


        }
    }
}

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Licenses
{
    public partial class frmRenewLicenseApplication : Form
    {

        private clsLicenseBusiness? _OldLicense;

        public frmRenewLicenseApplication()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void btnSearch_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(textBox2.Text))
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please enter a License ID to search.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            if (!int.TryParse(textBox2.Text.Trim(), out int LicenseID))
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please enter a valid numeric License ID.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            ucDriverLicenseInfo.LoadInfo(LicenseID);
            _OldLicense = null;

            if (ucDriverLicenseInfo.SelectedLicenseInfo == null)
                return;

            clsLicenseBusiness License = ucDriverLicenseInfo.SelectedLicenseInfo;

            if (!_IsEligibleForRenew(License, out string ErrorMessage))
            {
                MessageBox.Show(ErrorMessage, "Not Eligible", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            _OldLicense = License;
            ucApplicationNewLicenseInfo.LoadData(_OldLicense.LicenseID);

        }

        private bool _IsEligibleForRenew(clsLicenseBusiness License, out string ErrorMessage)
        {
            ErrorMessage = "";

            if (!License.IsActive)
            {
                ErrorMessage = "This license is not active and cannot be renewed.";
                return false;
            }

            if (License.ExpirationDate.Date > DateTime.Now.Date)
            {
                ErrorMessage = "This license has not expired yet. Only expired licenses can be renewed.";
                return false;
            }

            return true;
        }

        private void frmRenewLicenseApplication_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnSearch);
            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnRenew);
        }


        private void pnlTextBorder_Paint(object sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Panel pnl = (Panel)sender;
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;

            using (Pen pen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316"), 2))
            {
                Rectangle rect = new Rectangle(0, 0, pnl.Width - 1, pnl.Height - 1);
                using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = clsFormatHelper.GetRoundedRectanglePath(rect, 15))
                {
                    using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937")))
                    {
                        e.Graphics.FillPath(brush, path);
                    }
                    e.Graphics.DrawPath(pen, path);
                }
            }
        }

        private void btnRenew_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_OldLicense == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please search for an eligible (expired) license first.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            // Re-validate in case the state changed since the search.
            if (!_IsEligibleForRenew(_OldLicense, out string ErrorMessage))
            {
                MessageBox.Show(ErrorMessage, "Not Eligible", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            if (_OldLicense.LicenseClassInfo == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Could not load the license class information for this license.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            if (_OldLicense.DriverInfo == null || _OldLicense.DriverInfo.PersonInfo == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Could not load the driver/person information for this license.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            clsApplicationTypeBusiness ApplicationType = clsApplicationTypeBusiness.GetApplicationTypeInfoByID(2);
            decimal ApplicationFees = (ApplicationType != null) ? ApplicationType.ApplicationFees : 0;

            clsApplicationBusiness RenewApplication = new clsApplicationBusiness();
            RenewApplication.ApplicantPersonID = _OldLicense.DriverInfo.PersonInfo.PersonID;
            RenewApplication.ApplicationDate = DateTime.Now;
            RenewApplication.ApplicationTypeID = 2;
            RenewApplication.PaidFees = ApplicationFees;
            RenewApplication.CreatedByUserID = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.UserID;

            if (!RenewApplication.Save())
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Failed to create the renew application.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            clsLicenseBusiness NewLicense = new clsLicenseBusiness();
            NewLicense.ApplicationID = RenewApplication.ApplicationID;
            NewLicense.DriverID = _OldLicense.DriverID;
            NewLicense.LicenseClassID = _OldLicense.LicenseClassID;
            NewLicense.IssueDate = DateTime.Now;
            NewLicense.ExpirationDate = DateTime.Now.AddYears(_OldLicense.LicenseClassInfo.DefaultValidityLength);
            NewLicense.PaidFees = _OldLicense.LicenseClassInfo.ClassFees;
            NewLicense.IsActive = true;
            NewLicense.IssueReason = 2;
            NewLicense.CreatedByUserID = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.UserID;

            string UserNotes = ucApplicationNewLicenseInfo.Notes;
            string RenewalTrace = $"Renewed from License #{_OldLicense.LicenseID}.";
            NewLicense.Notes = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(UserNotes) ? RenewalTrace : $"{UserNotes} | {RenewalTrace}";

            if (!NewLicense.Save())
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Failed to issue the renewed license.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            _OldLicense.IsActive = false;
            _OldLicense.Save();

            RenewApplication.Completed();

            ucApplicationNewLicenseInfo.SetRenewalResult(RenewApplication.ApplicationID, NewLicense.LicenseID);
            ucDriverLicenseInfo.LoadInfo(_OldLicense.LicenseID);

            MessageBox.Show("Driving license renewed successfully.", "Success", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
        }

        private void ucApplicationNewLicenseInfo_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }
    }
}

}﻿using DVLD_Business;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Local_Driving_License_Applications
{
    public partial class frmAddApplicationInfo : Form
    {
        private clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness _LocalApp;

        public frmAddApplicationInfo(clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness LocalApp) {
            InitializeComponent();
            _LocalApp = LocalApp;
        }

        private void frmAddApplicationInfo_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            cmbFilterBy.FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat;
            cmbFilterBy.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            cmbFilterBy.ForeColor = Color.White;

            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnSave);

            _LoadComboBoxContent();
            _LoadData();

        }

        private void _LoadComboBoxContent()
        {
            DataTable dtClasses = clsLicenseClassBusiness.GetAllLicenseClasses();

            try
            {

                if (dtClasses.Rows.Count > 0)
                {
                    cmbFilterBy.DataSource = dtClasses;
                    cmbFilterBy.DisplayMember = "ClassName";
                    cmbFilterBy.ValueMember = "LicenseClassID";
                }
                else
                    MessageBox.Show("DataTable is null or empty. Check your database or business layer query.", "Debug", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);


            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Error Details: " + ex.Message, "Exception", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            }

        }

        private void _LoadData() {

            if (_LocalApp.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID != -1) {
                lblAnswerApplicationID.Text = _LocalApp.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID.ToString();
                lblAnswerApplicationDate.Text = _LocalApp.ApplicationInfo.ApplicationDate.ToShortDateString();
                cmbFilterBy.SelectedValue = _LocalApp.LicenseClassID;
                lblAnswerApplicationFees.Text = _LocalApp.ApplicationInfo.PaidFees.ToString();
                lblAnswerCreatedBy.Text = _LocalApp.ApplicationInfo.CreatedByUserID.ToString();
            }
            else
            {
                lblApplicationID.Text = "[???]";
                lblAnswerApplicationDate.Text = DateTime.Now.ToShortDateString();
                lblAnswerApplicationFees.Text = "15";
                lblAnswerCreatedBy.Text = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.UserID.ToString();
            }



        }

        private void btnSave_Click(object sender, EventArgs e) {
            int SelectedClassID = (int)cmbFilterBy.SelectedValue;

            if (_LocalApp.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID == -1) {

                if (clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness.DoesActiveApplicationExist(_LocalApp.ApplicationInfo.ApplicantPersonID, SelectedClassID))
                {
                    MessageBox.Show("This person already has an active (New or Completed) application for this license class.", "Duplicate Application", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                    return;
                }

                _LocalApp.ApplicationInfo.ApplicationDate = DateTime.Now;
                _LocalApp.ApplicationInfo.ApplicationTypeID = 1;
                _LocalApp.ApplicationInfo.ApplicationStatus = 1;
                _LocalApp.ApplicationInfo.LastStatusDate = DateTime.Now;
                _LocalApp.ApplicationInfo.PaidFees = Convert.ToDecimal(lblAnswerApplicationFees.Text);
                _LocalApp.ApplicationInfo.CreatedByUserID = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.UserID;
            }

            _LocalApp.LicenseClassID = SelectedClassID;

            if (_LocalApp.Save())
            {
                MessageBox.Show($"Application Saved! ID = {_LocalApp.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID}", "Success", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                lblApplicationID.Text = _LocalApp.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID.ToString();

                this.DialogResult = DialogResult.OK;
                this.Close();
            }

        }


    }

}

    public partial class frmAddUpdateLocalDrivingLicenseApplication : Form
    {
        public frmAddUpdateLocalDrivingLicenseApplication()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void frmManageLocalDrivingLicenseApplications_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnNext);
            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnSearch);
        }

        private void btnSearch_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (cmbFilterBy.SelectedItem == null) {
                MessageBox.Show("Please select a filter category first.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtSearchPerson.Text)){
                MessageBox.Show("Please enter a value to search.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            string? SelectedItem = cmbFilterBy.SelectedItem.ToString();
            string SearchValue = txtSearchPerson.Text.Trim();

            if (SelectedItem == "Person ID")
            {
                if (int.TryParse(SearchValue, out int PersonID))
                    ucShowPersonInfo.LoadPersonInfo(PersonID);
                else
                    MessageBox.Show("Please enter a valid numeric Person ID.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            }
            else if (SelectedItem == "National No.")
                ucShowPersonInfo.LoadPersonInfo(SearchValue);

        }


        private void pnlTextBorder_Paint(object sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Panel pnl = (Panel)sender;
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;

            using (Pen pen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316"), 2))
            {
                Rectangle rect = new Rectangle(0, 0, pnl.Width - 1, pnl.Height - 1);
                using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = clsFormatHelper.GetRoundedRectanglePath(rect, 15))
                {
                    using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937")))
                    {
                        e.Graphics.FillPath(brush, path);
                    }
                    e.Graphics.DrawPath(pen, path);
                }
            }
        }

        private void btnNext_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (ucShowPersonInfo.PersonID == -1)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please select a person first by searching before moving to the next step.", "Validation Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness NewApp = new clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness();
            NewApp.ApplicationInfo.ApplicantPersonID = ucShowPersonInfo.PersonID;

            frmAddApplicationInfo frm = new frmAddApplicationInfo(NewApp);
            frm.ShowDialog();

        }


    }
}

        private void _SetupTestSubMenus()
        {
            ScheduleVisionTestToolStripMenuItem = new ToolStripMenuItem("Schedule Vision Test");
            ScheduleWrittenTestToolStripMenuItem = new ToolStripMenuItem("Schedule Written Test");
            ScheduleStreetTestToolStripMenuItem = new ToolStripMenuItem("Schedule Street Test");

            ScheduleVisionTestToolStripMenuItem.Click += ScheduleVisionTestToolStripMenuItem_Click;
            ScheduleWrittenTestToolStripMenuItem.Click += ScheduleWrittenTestToolStripMenuItem_Click;
            ScheduleStreetTestToolStripMenuItem.Click += ScheduleStreetTestToolStripMenuItem_Click;

            ScheduleTestsToolStripMenuItem.DropDownItems.Clear();
            ScheduleTestsToolStripMenuItem.DropDownItems.Add(ScheduleVisionTestToolStripMenuItem);
            ScheduleTestsToolStripMenuItem.DropDownItems.Add(ScheduleWrittenTestToolStripMenuItem);
            ScheduleTestsToolStripMenuItem.DropDownItems.Add(ScheduleStreetTestToolStripMenuItem);
        }

        public frmManageLocalDrivingLicenseApplications()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void frmManageLocalDrivingLicenseApplications_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            txtSearchLocalDrivingLicenseApplication.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None;
            txtSearchLocalDrivingLicenseApplication.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            txtSearchLocalDrivingLicenseApplication.ForeColor = Color.White;
            txtSearchLocalDrivingLicenseApplication.PlaceholderText = "Search Employees...";
            txtSearchLocalDrivingLicenseApplication.TextAlign = HorizontalAlignment.Left;
            txtSearchLocalDrivingLicenseApplication.Top = (pnlTextBorder.Height - txtSearchLocalDrivingLicenseApplication.Height) / 2;  // ✅
            txtSearchLocalDrivingLicenseApplication.Left = 12;
            txtSearchLocalDrivingLicenseApplication.Width = pnlTextBorder.Width - 24;

            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnAddLocalDrivingLicenseApplication);

            cmbFilterBy.FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat;
            cmbFilterBy.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            cmbFilterBy.ForeColor = Color.White;

            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.BackColor = System.Drawing.ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#374151");
            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = Color.White;
            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionBackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316");
            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionForeColor = Color.White;

            clsFormatHelper.StyleDataGridView(dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications);

            if (cmbFilterBy.Items.Count > 0)
                cmbFilterBy.SelectedIndex = 0;

            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.AutoGenerateColumns = false;

            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.Columns["ApplicantPersonID"].Visible = false;

            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.Columns["LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID"].FillWeight = 5;
            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.Columns["ClassName"].FillWeight = 25;
            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.Columns["NationalNo"].FillWeight = 10;
            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.Columns["FullName"].FillWeight = 30;
            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.Columns["ApplicationDate"].FillWeight = 15;
            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.Columns["PassedTests"].FillWeight = 5;
            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.Columns["ApplicationStatus"].FillWeight = 10;

            _RefreshLocalDrivingLicenseApplications();
            _SetupTestSubMenus();
        }

        private void _RefreshLocalDrivingLicenseApplications()
        {
            _dtShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications = clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness.GetAllLocalDrivingLicenseApplications();
            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.DataSource = _dtShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications;
        }

        private void btnAddLocalDrivingLicenseApplication_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            frmAddUpdateLocalDrivingLicenseApplication frm = new frmAddUpdateLocalDrivingLicenseApplication();
            frm.ShowDialog();

            _RefreshLocalDrivingLicenseApplications();
        }

        private void txtSearchLocalDrivingLicenseApplication_TextChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (cmbFilterBy.SelectedItem == null)
                return;

            string? SelectedFilter = cmbFilterBy.SelectedItem.ToString();
            string FilterColumn = "";

            switch (SelectedFilter)
            {
                case "L.D.L.AppID":
                    FilterColumn = "LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID";
                    break;
                case "National No.":
                    FilterColumn = "NationalNo";
                    break;
                case "Full Name":
                    FilterColumn = "FullName";
                    break;
                case "Status":
                    FilterColumn = "ApplicationStatus";
                    break;
                default:
                    FilterColumn = "";
                    break;
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtSearchLocalDrivingLicenseApplication.Text) || FilterColumn == "")
            {
                _dtShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.DefaultView.RowFilter = "";
                return;
            }

            if (FilterColumn == "LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID")
            {
                if (int.TryParse(txtSearchLocalDrivingLicenseApplication.Text.Trim(), out int Result))
                    _dtShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.DefaultView.RowFilter = string.Format("[{0}] = {1}", FilterColumn, Result);
                else
                    _dtShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.DefaultView.RowFilter = "1=0";
            }
            else
            {
                _dtShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.DefaultView.RowFilter = string.Format("[{0}] LIKE '{1}%'", FilterColumn, txtSearchLocalDrivingLicenseApplication.Text.Trim());
            }


        }

        private void dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications_CellFormatting(object sender, DataGridViewCellFormattingEventArgs e)
        {

            if (e.RowIndex < 0 || e.Value == null)
                return;

            if (dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.Columns[e.ColumnIndex].Name == "ApplicationStatus")
            {
                switch (e.Value.ToString())
                {
                    case "1":
                        e.Value = "New";
                        break;
                    case "2":
                        e.Value = "Cancelled";
                        break;
                    case "3":
                        e.Value = "Completed";
                        break;
                }
            }

        }

        private void pnlTextBorder_Paint(object sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Panel pnl = (Panel)sender;
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;

            using (Pen pen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316"), 2))
            {
                Rectangle rect = new Rectangle(0, 0, pnl.Width - 1, pnl.Height - 1);
                using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = clsFormatHelper.GetRoundedRectanglePath(rect, 15))
                {

                    using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937")))
                    {
                        e.Graphics.FillPath(brush, path);
                    }
                    e.Graphics.DrawPath(pen, path);
                }
            }
        }

        private void cxmManageLocalDrivingLicenseApplications_Opening(object sender, CancelEventArgs e)
        {

            if (dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow == null)
            {
                e.Cancel = true;
                return;
            }

            int LocalAppID = (int)dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow.Cells["LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID"].Value;
            int PassedTests = Convert.ToInt32(dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow.Cells["PassedTests"].Value);
            string? Status = dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow.Cells["ApplicationStatus"].Value.ToString();

            bool IsNew = (Status.ToLower() == "new" || Status == "1");
            bool IsCompleted = (Status.ToLower() == "completed" || Status == "3");

            ShowApplicationInfoToolStripMenuItem.Enabled = true;
            ShowPersonLicenseHistoryToolStripMenuItem.Enabled = true;

            EditApplicationToolStripMenuItem.Enabled = IsNew && (PassedTests == 0);
            DeleteApplicationToolStripMenuItem.Enabled = IsNew;
            CancelApplicationToolStripMenuItem.Enabled = IsNew;

            bool HasNotPassedAllTests = (PassedTests < 3);
            ScheduleTestsToolStripMenuItem.Enabled = IsNew && HasNotPassedAllTests;

            if (ScheduleTestsToolStripMenuItem.Enabled)
            {
                ScheduleVisionTestToolStripMenuItem.Enabled = (PassedTests == 0);
                ScheduleWrittenTestToolStripMenuItem.Enabled = (PassedTests == 1);
                ScheduleStreetTestToolStripMenuItem.Enabled = (PassedTests == 2);
            }

            IssueDrivingLicenseToolStripMenuItem.Enabled = IsNew && (PassedTests == 3);

            ShowLicenseToolStripMenuItem.Enabled = IsCompleted;

        }

        private void ShowApplicationInfoToolStripMenuItem_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow == null)
                return;

            int LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID = (int)dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow.Cells["LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID"].Value;

            frmLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfo frm = new frmLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfo(LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID);
            frm.ShowDialog();

        }

        private void CancelApplicationToolStripMenuItem_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (MessageBox.Show("Are you sure you want to cancel this application?", "Confirm", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.Question) == DialogResult.No)
                return;

            int LocalAppID = (int)dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow.Cells["LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID"].Value;

            clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness LocalApp = clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness.GetLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfoByID(LocalAppID);

            if (LocalApp != null)
            {
                if (LocalApp.Cancel())
                {
                    MessageBox.Show("Application Cancelled Successfully.", "Cancelled", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);

                    _RefreshLocalDrivingLicenseApplications();
                }
                else
                    MessageBox.Show("Could not cancel application.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);


            }
        }

        private void DeleteApplicationToolStripMenuItem_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow == null)
                return;

            if (MessageBox.Show("Are you sure you want to delete this application?", "Confirm Delete", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.Warning) == DialogResult.No)
                return;

            int LocalAppID = (int)dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow.Cells["LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID"].Value;

            clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness? LocalApp = clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness.GetLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfoByID(LocalAppID);

            if (LocalApp != null)
            {

                if (LocalApp.Delete())
                {
                    MessageBox.Show("Application Deleted Successfully.", "Deleted", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                    _RefreshLocalDrivingLicenseApplications();
                }
                else
                    MessageBox.Show("Could not delete application, it may have related data (like tests).", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);

            }



        }

        private void EditApplicationToolStripMenuItem_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow == null)
                return;

            int LocalAppID = (int)dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow.Cells["LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID"].Value;

            clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness? LocalApp = clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness.GetLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfoByID(LocalAppID);

            if (LocalApp != null)
            {
                frmAddApplicationInfo frm = new frmAddApplicationInfo(LocalApp);
                frm.ShowDialog();

                _RefreshLocalDrivingLicenseApplications();
            }

        }

        private void ScheduleVisionTestToolStripMenuItem_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow == null)
                return;

            int LocalAppID = (int)dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow.Cells["LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID"].Value;

            frmTestAppointments frm = new frmTestAppointments(LocalAppID, 1);
            frm.ShowDialog();

            _RefreshLocalDrivingLicenseApplications();
        }

        private void ScheduleWrittenTestToolStripMenuItem_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow == null)
                return;

            int LocalAppID = (int)dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow.Cells["LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID"].Value;

            frmTestAppointments frm = new frmTestAppointments(LocalAppID, 2);
            frm.ShowDialog();

            _RefreshLocalDrivingLicenseApplications();
        }

        private void ScheduleStreetTestToolStripMenuItem_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow == null)
                return;

            int LocalAppID = (int)dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow.Cells["LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID"].Value;

            frmTestAppointments frm = new frmTestAppointments(LocalAppID, 3);
            frm.ShowDialog();

            _RefreshLocalDrivingLicenseApplications();


        }

        private void IssueDrivingLicenseToolStripMenuItem_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow == null)
                return;

            int LocalAppID = (int)dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow.Cells["LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID"].Value;

            frmIssueDriverLicenseFirstTime frm = new frmIssueDriverLicenseFirstTime(LocalAppID);
            frm.ShowDialog();

            _RefreshLocalDrivingLicenseApplications();

        }

        private void ShowLicenseToolStripMenuItem_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow == null)
                return;

            int LocalAppID = (int)dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow.Cells["LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID"].Value;

            clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness? LocalApp = clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness.GetLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfoByID(LocalAppID);

            if (LocalApp == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Application details could not be found.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            int LicenseID = LocalApp.GetActiveLicenseID();

            if (LicenseID != -1)
            {
                frmShowDriverLicenseInfo frm = new frmShowDriverLicenseInfo(LicenseID);
                frm.ShowDialog();
            }
            else
                MessageBox.Show("No active license found for this application.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);

        }

        private void ShowPersonLicenseHistoryToolStripMenuItem_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow == null)
                return;

            int PersonID = (int)dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow.Cells["ApplicantPersonID"].Value;

            frmShowPersonLicenseHistory frm = new frmShowPersonLicenseHistory(PersonID);
            frm.ShowDialog();

        }
    }
}

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Main_Menu
{
    public partial class frmApplicationsMainMenu : Form
    {
        public frmApplicationsMainMenu()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void frmApplicationsMainMenu_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _SetupPanelHoverEvents(this);

            clsFormatHelper.StyleContextMenu(cxmManageApplications);
            clsFormatHelper.StyleContextMenu(cxmDetainLicenses);
        }

        private void _SetupPanelHoverEvents(Control container)
        {
            foreach (Control ctrl in container.Controls)
            {
                if (ctrl is Panel pnl)
                {
                    pnl.BackColor = Color.FromArgb(12, 22, 50);
                    clsFormatHelper.SetControlsColor(pnl, textColor: Color.White, iconColor: Color.FromArgb(249, 115, 22));

                    pnl.MouseEnter += panel_MouseEnter;
                    pnl.MouseLeave += panel_MouseLeave;

                    foreach (Control child in pnl.Controls)
                    {
                        child.BackColor = Color.Transparent;
                        child.MouseEnter += panel_MouseEnter;
                        child.MouseLeave += panel_MouseLeave;
                    }
                }
                else if (ctrl.HasChildren)
                {
                    _SetupPanelHoverEvents(ctrl);
                }
            }
        }

        private void panel_MouseEnter(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            Panel? pnl = sender as Panel ?? (sender as Control)?.Parent as Panel;

            if (pnl != null)
            {
                pnl.BackColor = Color.FromArgb(249, 115, 22);
                clsFormatHelper.SetControlsColor(pnl, textColor: Color.White, iconColor: Color.White);
            }
        }

        private void panel_MouseLeave(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            Panel? pnl = sender as Panel ?? (sender as Control)?.Parent as Panel;

            if (pnl != null)
            {
                if (pnl.ClientRectangle.Contains(pnl.PointToClient(Cursor.Position)))
                    return;

                pnl.BackColor = Color.FromArgb(12, 22, 50);
                clsFormatHelper.SetControlsColor(pnl, textColor: Color.White, iconColor: Color.FromArgb(249, 115, 22));
            }
        }

        private void pnlDetainLicenses_MouseEnter(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            panel_MouseEnter(sender, e);

            Panel? pnl = sender as Panel ?? (sender as Control)?.Parent as Panel;

            if (pnl == null)
                return;

            if (!cxmDetainLicenses.Visible)
                cxmDetainLicenses.Show(pnl, new Point(pnl.Width, 0));
        }

        private void pnlManageApplications_MouseEnter(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            panel_MouseEnter(sender, e);

            Panel? pnl = sender as Panel ?? (sender as Control)?.Parent as Panel;

            if (pnl == null)
                return;

            if (!cxmManageApplications.Visible)
                cxmManageApplications.Show(pnl, new Point(pnl.Width, 0));
        }

        private void pnlDrivingLicenseServices_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            frmDrivingLicenseServicesMainMenu frm = new frmDrivingLicenseServicesMainMenu();
            frm.ShowDialog();
        }

        private void toolStripMenuItemLocalLicense_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

using System.Collections.Generic;

using System.Drawing;

using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Main_Menu
{
    public partial class frmDrivingLicenseServicesMainMenu : Form
    {
        public frmDrivingLicenseServicesMainMenu()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            clsFormatHelper.StyleContextMenu(cxmNewDrivingLicense);
        }

        private void frmDrivingLicenseServicesMainMenu_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _SetupPanelHoverEvents(this);
        }

        private void _SetupPanelHoverEvents(Control container)
        {
            foreach (Control ctrl in container.Controls)
            {
                if (ctrl is Panel pnl)
                {
                    pnl.BackColor = Color.FromArgb(12, 22, 50);
                    clsFormatHelper.SetControlsColor(pnl, textColor: Color.White, iconColor: Color.FromArgb(249, 115, 22));

                    pnl.MouseEnter += panel_MouseEnter;
                    pnl.MouseLeave += panel_MouseLeave;

                    foreach (Control child in pnl.Controls)
                    {
                        child.BackColor = Color.Transparent;
                        child.MouseEnter += panel_MouseEnter;
                        child.MouseLeave += panel_MouseLeave;
                    }
                }
                else if (ctrl.HasChildren)
                {
                    _SetupPanelHoverEvents(ctrl);
                }
            }
        }

        private void panel_MouseEnter(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            Panel? pnl = sender as Panel ?? (sender as Control)?.Parent as Panel;

            if (pnl != null)
            {
                pnl.BackColor = Color.FromArgb(249, 115, 22);
                clsFormatHelper.SetControlsColor(pnl, textColor: Color.White, iconColor: Color.White);
            }
        }

        private void panel_MouseLeave(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            Panel? pnl = sender as Panel ?? (sender as Control)?.Parent as Panel;

            if (pnl != null)
            {
                if (pnl.ClientRectangle.Contains(pnl.PointToClient(Cursor.Position)))
                    return;

                pnl.BackColor = Color.FromArgb(12, 22, 50);
                clsFormatHelper.SetControlsColor(pnl, textColor: Color.White, iconColor: Color.FromArgb(249, 115, 22));
            }
        }

        private void pnlNewDrivingLicense_MouseEnter(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            panel_MouseEnter(sender, e);

            Panel? pnl = sender as Panel ?? (sender as Control)?.Parent as Panel;

            if (pnl == null)
                return;

            if (!cxmNewDrivingLicense.Visible)
                cxmNewDrivingLicense.Show(pnl, new Point(pnl.Width, 0));
        }

        private void cxmNewDrivingLicense_Opening(object sender, CancelEventArgs e)
        {

        }

        private void toolStripMenuItemLocalLicense_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            frmAddUpdateLocalDrivingLicenseApplication frm = new frmAddUpdateLocalDrivingLicenseApplication();
            frm.ShowDialog();
        }

        private void pnlRenewDrivingLicense_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            frmRenewLicenseApplication frm = new frmRenewLicenseApplication();
            frm.ShowDialog();
        }

        private void pnlReplacementForLostOrDamagedLicense_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            frmLicenseReplacement frm = new frmLicenseReplacement();

        }

}﻿using DVLD_Presentation.Drivers;
using DVLD_Presentation.Main_Menu;
using DVLD_Presentation.People;
using DVLD_Presentation.Users;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation
{
    public partial class frmMainMenu : Form
    {
        public frmMainMenu()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void frmMainMenu_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _SetupPanelHoverEvents(this);
        }

        private void _SetupPanelHoverEvents(Control container)
        {
            foreach (Control ctrl in container.Controls)
            {
                if (ctrl is Panel pnl && pnl != pnlSeparator && pnl != pnlCurrentUser)
                {
                    pnl.BackColor = Color.FromArgb(12, 22, 50);
                    clsFormatHelper.SetControlsColor(pnl, textColor: Color.White, iconColor: Color.FromArgb(249, 115, 22));

                    pnl.MouseEnter += panel_MouseEnter;
                    pnl.MouseLeave += panel_MouseLeave;

                    foreach (Control child in pnl.Controls)
                    {
                        child.BackColor = Color.Transparent;
                        child.MouseEnter += panel_MouseEnter;
                        child.MouseLeave += panel_MouseLeave;
                    }
                }
                else if (ctrl.HasChildren)
                {
                    _SetupPanelHoverEvents(ctrl);
                }
            }
        }

        private void panel_MouseEnter(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            Panel? pnl = sender as Panel ?? (sender as Control)?.Parent as Panel;

            if (pnl != null)
            {
                pnl.BackColor = Color.FromArgb(249, 115, 22);
                clsFormatHelper.SetControlsColor(pnl, textColor: Color.White, iconColor: Color.White);
            }
        }

        private void panel_MouseLeave(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            Panel? pnl = sender as Panel ?? (sender as Control)?.Parent as Panel;

            if (pnl != null)
            {
                if (pnl.ClientRectangle.Contains(pnl.PointToClient(Cursor.Position)))
                    return;

                pnl.BackColor = Color.FromArgb(12, 22, 50);
                clsFormatHelper.SetControlsColor(pnl, textColor: Color.White, iconColor: Color.FromArgb(249, 115, 22));
            }
        }

        private void pnlSeparator_Paint(object sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            _DrawVerticalSeparator((Panel)sender, e);
        }

        private void pnlCurrentUser_Paint(object sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            _DrawVerticalSeparator((Panel)sender, e);
        }

        private void _DrawVerticalSeparator(Panel pnl, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;

            Color transparent = Color.Transparent;
            Color lineColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#4B5563");

            using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.LinearGradientBrush brush =
                new System.Drawing.Drawing2D.LinearGradientBrush(
                    pnl.ClientRectangle,
                    transparent,
                    transparent,
                    System.Drawing.Drawing2D.LinearGradientMode.Vertical))
            {
                System.Drawing.Drawing2D.ColorBlend colorBlend = new System.Drawing.Drawing2D.ColorBlend(3);
                colorBlend.Colors = new Color[] { transparent, lineColor, transparent };
                colorBlend.Positions = new float[] { 0.0f, 0.5f, 1.0f };

                brush.InterpolationColors = colorBlend;

                int centerX = pnl.Width / 2;
                using (Pen pen = new Pen(brush, 1.5f))
                {
                    e.Graphics.DrawLine(pen, centerX, 0, centerX, pnl.Height);
                }
            }
        }

        private void lblCurrentUser_MouseEnter(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            lblCurrentUser.ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(249, 115, 22);
            lblCurrentUser.Cursor = Cursors.Hand;

            if (!cxmUserMenu.Visible)
                cxmUserMenu.Show(lblCurrentUser, new Point(0, lblCurrentUser.Height));
        }

        private void lblCurrentUser_MouseLeave(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            lblCurrentUser.ForeColor = Color.White;
        }

        private void pnlUsers_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            frmManageUsers frm = new frmManageUsers();
            frm.ShowDialog();
        }

        private void pnlPeople_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

namespace DVLD_Presentation.People
{
    public partial class frmAddUpdatePerson : Form
    {

        private clsPersonBusiness? _Person;

        private void _LoadData() {
            
            if (_Person == null)
                return;

            if (_Person.Mode == clsPersonBusiness.enMode.AddNew) {
                btnSave.Text = "Add";
                return;
            }

            btnSave.Text = "Update";

            txtFirstName.Text = _Person.FirstName;
            txtSecondName.Text = _Person.SecondName;
            txtThirdName.Text = _Person.ThirdName;
            txtLastName.Text = _Person.LastName;

            txtNationalNo.Text = _Person.NationalNo;
            timeDateOfBirth.Value = _Person.DateOfBirth;
            txtPhone.Text = _Person.Phone;
            txtEmail.Text = _Person.Email;
            txtAddress.Text = _Person.Address;

            if (_Person.Gendor == 0)
                rbMale.Checked = true;
            else
                rbFemale.Checked = true;

            cmbCountry.SelectedValue = _Person.NationalityCountryID;

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Person.ImagePath) &&
                File.Exists(_Person.ImagePath))
            {
                pbProfilePicture.Image = Image.FromFile(_Person.ImagePath);
                pbProfilePicture.Tag = _Person.ImagePath;
            }
        }
        
        public frmAddUpdatePerson()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _Person = new clsPersonBusiness();
        }

        public frmAddUpdatePerson(int PersonID)
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _Person = clsPersonBusiness.Find(PersonID);

            if (_Person == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("This form will be closed because Person ID is not found.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                this.Close();
            }


        }

        private void frmAddUpdatePerson_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _FillCountriesInComboBox();
            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnSave);
            clsFormatHelper.StyleDateTimePicker(timeDateOfBirth);
            _LoadData();

        }

        private void rbGender_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (pbProfilePicture.Tag == null)
                if (rbMale.Checked)
                    pbProfilePicture.Image = Properties.Resources.male;
                else if (rbFemale.Checked)
                    pbProfilePicture.Image = Properties.Resources.female;
        }

        private void pbProfilePicture_Click(object sender, EventArgs e) {
            
            OpenFileDialog MyFileDialog = new OpenFileDialog();

            MyFileDialog.Filter = "Image Files (*.jpg; *.jpeg; *.png; *.gif; *.bmp)|*.jpg; *.jpeg; *.png; *.gif; *.bmp";
            MyFileDialog.Title = "Select Your Profile Picture";

            if (MyFileDialog.ShowDialog() == DialogResult.OK)
            {
                pbProfilePicture.Image = Image.FromFile(MyFileDialog.FileName);
                pbProfilePicture.Tag = MyFileDialog.FileName;
            }
        }

        private void tsmRemovePicture_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            pbProfilePicture.Tag = null;

            if (rbMale.Checked)
                pbProfilePicture.Image = Properties.Resources.male;
            else if (rbFemale.Checked)
                pbProfilePicture.Image = Properties.Resources.female;
            else
                pbProfilePicture.Image = null;

        }

        private void btnSave_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (!_ValidateRequiredFields()) {
                MessageBox.Show("Please fill all required fields.", "Validation Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            if (_Person == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Error: Person object is null.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            if (!this.ValidateChildren())
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Some fields are not valid, please check the red error icons.", "Validation Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            if (cmbCountry.SelectedValue == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please select a valid country.", "Validation Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            _Person.FirstName = txtFirstName.Text.Trim();
            _Person.SecondName = txtSecondName.Text.Trim();
            _Person.ThirdName = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtThirdName.Text) ? null : txtThirdName.Text.Trim();
            _Person.LastName = txtLastName.Text.Trim();

            _Person.NationalNo = txtNationalNo.Text.Trim();
            _Person.DateOfBirth = timeDateOfBirth.Value;
            _Person.Gendor = rbMale.Checked ? (byte)0 : (byte)1;
            _Person.Phone = txtPhone.Text.Trim();
            _Person.Email = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtEmail.Text) ? null : txtEmail.Text.Trim();

            _Person.NationalityCountryID = Convert.ToInt32(cmbCountry.SelectedValue);
            _Person.Address = txtAddress.Text.Trim();

            _Person.ImagePath = pbProfilePicture.Tag?.ToString();

            if (pbProfilePicture.Tag != null)
                _Person.ImagePath = pbProfilePicture.Tag.ToString();
            else
                _Person.ImagePath = null;


            if (_Person.Save())
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Data Saved Successfully.", "Saved", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                btnSave.Text = "Update";
            }
            else
                MessageBox.Show("Error: Data Is not Saved Successfully.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);


        }

        private void _FillCountriesInComboBox()
        {
            DataTable dtCountries = clsPersonBusiness.GetAllCountries();

            try
            {

                if (dtCountries.Rows.Count > 0)
                {
                    cmbCountry.DataSource = dtCountries;
                    cmbCountry.DisplayMember = "CountryName";
                    cmbCountry.ValueMember = "CountryID";
                }
                else
                    MessageBox.Show("DataTable is null or empty. Check your database or business layer query.", "Debug", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);

            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Error Details: " + ex.Message, "Exception", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            }

            cmbCountry.SelectedIndex = cmbCountry.FindString("Jordan");
        }

        private void grpGender_Paint(object sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            GroupBox box = sender as GroupBox;
            if (box == null) return;

            e.Graphics.Clear(box.BackColor);

            TextRenderer.DrawText(e.Graphics, box.Text, box.Font, new Point(0, 0), box.ForeColor);
        }

        private void txtNationalNo_Validating(object sender, CancelEventArgs e)
        {
            string EnteredNationalNo = txtNationalNo.Text.Trim();

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(EnteredNationalNo))
            {
                e.Cancel = true; 
                errUniqueNationalNo.SetError(txtNationalNo, "This field is required!");
                return; 
            }

            if (_Person.Mode == clsPersonBusiness.enMode.Update && EnteredNationalNo == _Person.NationalNo)
            {
                e.Cancel = false;
                errUniqueNationalNo.SetError(txtNationalNo, "");
                return;
            }

            if (clsPersonBusiness.IsPersonExist(EnteredNationalNo))
            {
                e.Cancel = true; 
                errUniqueNationalNo.SetError(txtNationalNo, "This National Number is already in use!");
            }
            else
            {
                e.Cancel = false;
                errUniqueNationalNo.SetError(txtNationalNo, "");
            }
        }

        private void RequiredField_Validating(object sender, CancelEventArgs e) {
            UITextBox txt = (UITextBox)sender;

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txt.Text)) 
                txt.RectColor = Color.Red;
            else
                txt.RectColor = Color.FromArgb(80, 160, 255); 
        }

        private bool _ValidateRequiredFields() {
            
            bool IsValid = true;
            UITextBox FirstInvalidTxt = null;

            UITextBox[] RequiredFields = { txtFirstName, txtSecondName, txtLastName, txtPhone, txtAddress };

            foreach (UITextBox txt in RequiredFields) {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txt.Text)) {
                    txt.RectColor = Color.Red;
                    IsValid = false;

                    if (FirstInvalidTxt == null)
                        FirstInvalidTxt = txt;
                }
                else
                    txt.RectColor = Color.FromArgb(80, 160, 255);
            }

            if (!IsValid && FirstInvalidTxt != null)
                FirstInvalidTxt.Focus();

            return IsValid;
        }
    }
}
