        private void frmManagePeople_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            dgvShowPeople.AutoGenerateColumns = false;
            _RefreshPeopleList();



            this.BackColor = System.Drawing.Color.FromArgb(31, 41, 55);

            txtSearchPerson.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None;
            txtSearchPerson.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            txtSearchPerson.ForeColor = Color.White;
            txtSearchPerson.PlaceholderText = "Search Employees...";

            txtSearchPerson.TextAlign = HorizontalAlignment.Left;
            txtSearchPerson.Top = (pnlTextBorder.Height - txtSearchPerson.Height) / 2;
            txtSearchPerson.Left = 12;
            txtSearchPerson.Width = pnlTextBorder.Width - 24;

            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnAddPerson);
            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnUpdatePerson);
            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnDeletePerson);


            cmbFilterBy.FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat;
            cmbFilterBy.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            cmbFilterBy.ForeColor = Color.White;


            dgvShowPeople.BackColor = System.Drawing.ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            dgvShowPeople.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#374151");
            dgvShowPeople.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = Color.White;
            dgvShowPeople.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionBackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316");
            dgvShowPeople.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionForeColor = Color.White;


            clsFormatHelper.StyleDataGridView(dgvShowPeople);

            if (cmbFilterBy.Items.Count > 0)
                cmbFilterBy.SelectedIndex = 0;

        }



        private void btnDeletePerson_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (dgvShowPeople.SelectedRows.Count > 0) {
                int PersonID = Convert.ToInt32(dgvShowPeople.SelectedRows[0].Cells["PersonID"].Value);

                if (MessageBox.Show("Are you sure you want to proceed with the deletion process?", "Confirm Delete", MessageBoxButtons.OKCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Question) == DialogResult.OK)
                {
                    if (clsPersonBusiness.Delete(PersonID))
                    {
                        MessageBox.Show("Person Deleted Successfully.", "Successful", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                        _RefreshPeopleList();
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        MessageBox.Show("Person was not deleted due to data linked to it.", "Failed", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        private void pnlTextBorder_Paint(object sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Panel pnl = (Panel)sender;
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;

            using (Pen pen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316"), 2))
            {
                Rectangle rect = new Rectangle(0, 0, pnl.Width - 1, pnl.Height - 1);
                using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = clsFormatHelper.GetRoundedRectanglePath(rect, 15))
                {

                    using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937")))
                    {
                        e.Graphics.FillPath(brush, path);
                    }
                    e.Graphics.DrawPath(pen, path);
                }
            }
        }

        private void txtSearchPerson_TextChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            string SelectedFilter = cmbFilterBy.SelectedItem.ToString();
            string FilterColumn = "";

            switch (SelectedFilter)
            {
                case "Person ID":
                    FilterColumn = "PersonID";
                    break;
                case "National No.":
                    FilterColumn = "NationalNo";
                    break;
                case "First Name":
                    FilterColumn = "FirstName";
                    break;
                case "Second Name":
                    FilterColumn = "SecondName";
                    break;
                case "Third Name":
                    FilterColumn = "ThirdName";
                    break;
                case "Last Name":
                    FilterColumn = "LastName";
                    break;
                case "Nationality":
                    FilterColumn = "Nationality";
                    break;
                case "Gender":
                    FilterColumn = "Gendor";
                    break;
                case "Phone":
                    FilterColumn = "Phone";
                    break;
                case "Email":
                    FilterColumn = "Email";
                    break;
                default:
                    FilterColumn = "";
                    break;
            }

            if (txtSearchPerson.Text.Trim() == "")
            {
                _dtAllPeople.DefaultView.RowFilter = "";
                return;
            }

            if (FilterColumn == "")
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Invalid filter selected", "Error");
                return;
            }

            if (FilterColumn == "PersonID")
            {
                if (int.TryParse(txtSearchPerson.Text.Trim(), out int Result))
                    _dtAllPeople.DefaultView.RowFilter = string.Format("[{0}] = {1}", FilterColumn, Result);   
                else
                    _dtAllPeople.DefaultView.RowFilter = "1=0"; 
            }
            else if (FilterColumn == "Gendor")
            {
                string Input = txtSearchPerson.Text.Trim().ToUpper();

                if (Input == "M" || Input == "MALE")
                    _dtAllPeople.DefaultView.RowFilter = "Gendor = 0";
                else if (Input == "F" || Input == "FEMALE")
                    _dtAllPeople.DefaultView.RowFilter = "Gendor = 1";
                else
                    _dtAllPeople.DefaultView.RowFilter = "1=0"; 
            }
            else if (FilterColumn == "Nationality")
            {
                _dtAllPeople.DefaultView.RowFilter = string.Format("[{0}] LIKE '{1}%'", FilterColumn, txtSearchPerson.Text.Trim());
            }
            else      
                _dtAllPeople.DefaultView.RowFilter = string.Format("[{0}] LIKE '{1}%'", FilterColumn, txtSearchPerson.Text.Trim());
            
        }


        private void btnAddPerson_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            frmAddUpdatePerson frm = new frmAddUpdatePerson();
            frm.ShowDialog();
            _RefreshPeopleList();

        }

        private void btnUpdatePerson_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (dgvShowPeople.SelectedRows.Count > 0)
            {
                int PersonID = Convert.ToInt32(dgvShowPeople.SelectedRows[0].Cells["PersonID"].Value);

                frmAddUpdatePerson frm = new frmAddUpdatePerson(PersonID);
                frm.ShowDialog();
                _RefreshPeopleList();
            }
            else
                MessageBox.Show("Please select a person from the list first.", "Selection Required", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
        }

        private void _RefreshPeopleList()
        {
            _dtAllPeople = clsPersonBusiness.GetAllPeople();
            dgvShowPeople.DataSource = _dtAllPeople;
        }

        private void cmbFilterBy_SelectedIndexChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            txtSearchPerson.Visible = (cmbFilterBy.SelectedIndex != 0);
            pnlTextBorder.Visible = (cmbFilterBy.SelectedIndex != 0);

            if (txtSearchPerson.Visible)
            {
                txtSearchPerson.Text = "";
                txtSearchPerson.Focus();
            }

        }

        private void dgvShowPeople_CellFormatting(object sender, DataGridViewCellFormattingEventArgs e) {
            
            if (e.RowIndex < 0 || e.Value == null) 
                return;

            if (dgvShowPeople.Columns[e.ColumnIndex].Name == "Gendor")
            {
                e.Value = (e.Value.ToString() == "0") ? "M" : "F";
            }

            if (dgvShowPeople.Columns[e.ColumnIndex].Name == "Nationality")
            {
                if (int.TryParse(e.Value.ToString(), out int id))
                    e.Value = clsPersonBusiness.GetCountryNameByID(id);
                
            }
        }

    }
}

        [global::System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.SuppressMessageAttribute("Microsoft.Performance", "CA1811:AvoidUncalledPrivateCode")]
        internal Resources() {
        }
        
        /// <summary>
        ///   Returns the cached ResourceManager instance used by this class.
        /// </summary>
        [global::System.ComponentModel.EditorBrowsableAttribute(global::System.ComponentModel.EditorBrowsableState.Advanced)]
        internal static global::System.Resources.ResourceManager ResourceManager {
            get {
                if (object.ReferenceEquals(resourceMan, null)) {
                    global::System.Resources.ResourceManager temp = new global::System.Resources.ResourceManager("DVLD_Presentation.Properties.Resources", typeof(Resources).Assembly);
                    resourceMan = temp;
                }
                return resourceMan;
            }
        }
        
        /// <summary>
        ///   Overrides the current thread's CurrentUICulture property for all
        ///   resource lookups using this strongly typed resource class.
        /// </summary>
        [global::System.ComponentModel.EditorBrowsableAttribute(global::System.ComponentModel.EditorBrowsableState.Advanced)]
        internal static global::System.Globalization.CultureInfo Culture {
            get {
                return resourceCulture;
            }
            set {
                resourceCulture = value;
            }
        }
        
        /// <summary>
        ///   Looks up a localized resource of type System.Drawing.Bitmap.
        /// </summary>
        internal static System.Drawing.Bitmap female {
            get {
                object obj = ResourceManager.GetObject("female", resourceCulture);
                return ((System.Drawing.Bitmap)(obj));
            }
        }
        
        /// <summary>
        ///   Looks up a localized resource of type System.Drawing.Bitmap.
        /// </summary>
        internal static System.Drawing.Bitmap male {
            get {
                object obj = ResourceManager.GetObject("male", resourceCulture);
                return ((System.Drawing.Bitmap)(obj));
            }
        }
    }

﻿using DVLD_Business;
using DVLD_Presentation.Application_Types;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Test_Types {
    public partial class frmManageTestTypes : Form
    {

        private DataTable _dtShowTestTypes;


        public frmManageTestTypes()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void frmManageTestTypes_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            dgvShowTestTypes.BackColor = System.Drawing.ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            dgvShowTestTypes.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#374151");
            dgvShowTestTypes.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = Color.White;
            dgvShowTestTypes.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionBackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316");
            dgvShowTestTypes.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionForeColor = Color.White;

            clsFormatHelper.StyleDataGridView(dgvShowTestTypes);
            dgvShowTestTypes.AutoGenerateColumns = false;

            dgvShowTestTypes.AutoSizeColumnsMode = DataGridViewAutoSizeColumnsMode.Fill;

            dgvShowTestTypes.Columns[0].FillWeight = 5;
            dgvShowTestTypes.Columns[1].FillWeight = 15;
            dgvShowTestTypes.Columns[2].FillWeight = 71;
            dgvShowTestTypes.Columns[3].FillWeight = 9;

            _RefreshTestTypes();
        }



        private void _RefreshTestTypes()
        {
            _dtShowTestTypes = clsTestTypeBusiness.GetAllTestTypes();
            dgvShowTestTypes.DataSource = _dtShowTestTypes;
            lblRecordsNumber.Text = _dtShowTestTypes.Rows.Count.ToString();
        }

        private void dgvShowTestTypes_CellDoubleClick(object sender, DataGridViewCellEventArgs e) {
            
            if (e.RowIndex < 0)
                return;

            int TestTypeID = (int)dgvShowTestTypes.CurrentRow.Cells["TestTypeID"].Value;

            frmUpdateTestType frm = new frmUpdateTestType(TestTypeID);
            frm.ShowDialog();

            _RefreshTestTypes();
        }
    }
}

}﻿using DVLD_Business;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Test_Types
{
    public partial class frmUpdateTestType : Form
    {
        private int _TestTypeID = -1;
        private clsTestTypeBusiness? _TestType;
        public frmUpdateTestType(int TestTypeID)
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _TestTypeID = TestTypeID;
        }

        private void _LoadData()
        {
            _TestType = clsTestTypeBusiness.GetTestTypeInfoByID(_TestTypeID);

            if (_TestType == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Test Type not found.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                this.Close();
                return;
            }

            lblAnswerID.Text = Convert.ToString(_TestType.TestTypeID);
            txtTitle.Text = _TestType.TestTypeTitle;
            txtDescription.Text = _TestType.TestTypeDescription;
            txtFees.Text = Convert.ToString(_TestType.TestTypeFees);

        }

        private void frmUpdateTestType_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _LoadData();
        }

        private void btnSave_Click(object sender, EventArgs e) {
            
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtTitle.Text)) {
                MessageBox.Show("Title cannot be empty.", "Validation Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                txtTitle.Focus();
                return;
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtDescription.Text)) {
                MessageBox.Show("Description cannot be empty.", "Validation Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                txtDescription.Focus();
                return;
            }

            if (!decimal.TryParse(txtFees.Text.Trim(), out decimal Fees) || Fees < 0) {
                MessageBox.Show("Please enter a valid positive number for fees.", "Validation Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                txtFees.Focus();
                return;
            }

            _TestType.TestTypeTitle = txtTitle.Text.Trim();
            _TestType.TestTypeDescription = txtDescription.Text.Trim();
            _TestType.TestTypeFees = Fees;
            
            if(_TestType.UpdateTestType()) {
                MessageBox.Show("Data Updated Successfully.", "Saved", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                this.Close();
            }
            else
                MessageBox.Show("Error: Data was not updated.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);

        }
    }
}

}﻿using DVLD_Business;
using System;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Tests
{
    public partial class frmAddUpdateTest : Form
    {
        private clsTestAppointmentBusiness? _Appointment;
        private int _TrialCount = 0;

        public frmAddUpdateTest(int LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID, int TestTypeID)
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            _Appointment = new clsTestAppointmentBusiness();
            _Appointment.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID = LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID;
            _Appointment.TestTypeID = TestTypeID;
        }

        public frmAddUpdateTest(int TestAppointmentID)
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _Appointment = clsTestAppointmentBusiness.GetTestAppointmentInfoByID(TestAppointmentID);
        }

        private void frmAddTest_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (_Appointment == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Error: Appointment data is missing!", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                this.Close();
                return;
            }

            clsFormatHelper.StyleDateTimePicker(ucShowTestInfo.TestDatePicker);

            _TrialCount = ucShowTestInfo.LoadData(_Appointment.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID, _Appointment.TestTypeID);

            if (_Appointment.TestAppointmentID == -1)
                _LoadFeesAndRetakeInfoForNewAppointment();
            else
            {
                ucShowTestInfo.TestDatePicker.Value = _Appointment.AppointmentDate;

                pnlRetakeTest.Enabled = false;
                lblAnswerTotalFees.Text = _Appointment.PaidFees.ToString("0.00");
                lblAnswerRetakeApplicationFees.Text = "0.00";

                if (_Appointment.RetakeTestApplicationID != -1)
                    lblAnswerRetakeTestApplicationID.Text = _Appointment.RetakeTestApplicationID.ToString();
                else
                    lblAnswerRetakeTestApplicationID.Text = "N/A";
            }
        }

        private void _LoadFeesAndRetakeInfoForNewAppointment()
        {

            decimal TestFees = 0;
            clsTestTypeBusiness? TestType = clsTestTypeBusiness.GetTestTypeInfoByID(_Appointment.TestTypeID);

            if (TestType != null)
                TestFees = TestType.TestTypeFees;

            if (_TrialCount > 0)
            {
                pnlRetakeTest.Enabled = true;

                clsApplicationTypeBusiness? RetakeAppType = clsApplicationTypeBusiness.GetApplicationTypeInfoByID(7);
                decimal RetakeAppFees = RetakeAppType != null ? RetakeAppType.ApplicationFees : 0;

                lblAnswerRetakeApplicationFees.Text = RetakeAppFees.ToString("0.00");
                lblAnswerTotalFees.Text = (TestFees + RetakeAppFees).ToString("0.00");
                lblAnswerRetakeTestApplicationID.Text = "N/A";
            }
            else
            {
                pnlRetakeTest.Enabled = false;
                lblAnswerRetakeApplicationFees.Text = "0.00";
                lblAnswerTotalFees.Text = TestFees.ToString("0.00");
                lblAnswerRetakeTestApplicationID.Text = "N/A";
            }
        }

        private void btnAdd_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (_Appointment.TestAppointmentID == -1 && _TrialCount > 0)
            {
                clsApplicationBusiness RetakeApp = new clsApplicationBusiness();

                RetakeApp.ApplicantPersonID = clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness.GetLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfoByID(_Appointment.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID).ApplicationInfo.ApplicantPersonID;
                RetakeApp.ApplicationDate = DateTime.Now;
                RetakeApp.ApplicationTypeID = 7;
                RetakeApp.ApplicationStatus = 3;
                RetakeApp.LastStatusDate = DateTime.Now;
                RetakeApp.PaidFees = Convert.ToDecimal(lblAnswerRetakeApplicationFees.Text);

                // <-- تم تعديلها لتأخذ الآي دي الخاص بالمستخدم الحالي
                RetakeApp.CreatedByUserID = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.UserID;

                if (!RetakeApp.Save())
                {
                    MessageBox.Show("Failed to create Retake Application", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                    return;
                }

                _Appointment.RetakeTestApplicationID = RetakeApp.ApplicationID;
            }

            _Appointment.AppointmentDate = ucShowTestInfo.TestDatePicker.Value;
            _Appointment.PaidFees = Convert.ToDecimal(lblAnswerTotalFees.Text);

            _Appointment.CreatedByUserID = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.UserID;

            if (_Appointment.Save())
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Appointment Saved Successfully!", "Success", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                this.Close();
            }
            else
                MessageBox.Show("Failed to save Appointment", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
        }

        private void ucShowTestInfo_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
        }
    }

    public partial class frmIssueDriverLicenseFirstTime : Form
    {
        private int _LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID = -1;
        private clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness? _LocalDrivingLicenseApplication;
        private int _CreatedLicenseID = -1;

        public frmIssueDriverLicenseFirstTime(int LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID)
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID = LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID;
        }

        private void frmIssueDriverLicenseFirstTime_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _LocalDrivingLicenseApplication = clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness.GetLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfoByID(_LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID);

            if (_LocalDrivingLicenseApplication == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("No Application with ID = " + _LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID, "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                this.Close();
                return;
            }

            if (_LocalDrivingLicenseApplication.GetPassedTestCount() < 3)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Person should pass all tests first.", "Not Allowed", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                this.Close();
                return;
            }

            ucLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfo.LoadLocalApplicationData(_LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID);
            ucApplicationBasicInfo.LoadApplicationData(_LocalDrivingLicenseApplication.ApplicationID);
            ucLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfo.EnableShowLicense = false;
        }

        private void btnIssue_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MessageBox.Show("Are you sure you want to issue the license for this application?", "Confirm", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.Question) == DialogResult.No)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (_LocalDrivingLicenseApplication == null || _LocalDrivingLicenseApplication.ApplicationInfo == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Application details are missing.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            // 1. الوصول لرقم الشخص من خلال الكائن الفرعي ApplicationInfo
            int ApplicantPersonID = _LocalDrivingLicenseApplication.ApplicationInfo.ApplicantPersonID;

            // 2. البحث عن السائق أو إنشاء سجل جديد
            clsDriverBusiness? Driver = clsDriverBusiness.GetDriverInfoByPersonID(ApplicantPersonID);

            if (Driver == null)
            {
                Driver = new clsDriverBusiness();
                Driver.PersonID = ApplicantPersonID;
                Driver.CreatedByUserID = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.UserID;
                Driver.CreatedDate = DateTime.Now;

                if (!Driver.Save())
                {
                    MessageBox.Show("Failed to create a new driver record.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                    return;
                }
            }

            // 3. جلب بيانات صنف الرخصة بشكل مستقل لمعرفة مدة الصلاحية والرسوم
            clsLicenseClassBusiness? LicenseClass = clsLicenseClassBusiness.GetLicenseClassInfoByID(_LocalDrivingLicenseApplication.LicenseClassID);

            if (LicenseClass == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("License class details could not be found.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            // 4. تجهيز بيانات الرخصة الجديدة
            clsLicenseBusiness License = new clsLicenseBusiness();
            License.ApplicationID = _LocalDrivingLicenseApplication.ApplicationID;
            License.DriverID = Driver.DriverID;
            License.LicenseClassID = _LocalDrivingLicenseApplication.LicenseClassID;
            License.IssueDate = DateTime.Now;
            License.ExpirationDate = DateTime.Now.AddYears(LicenseClass.DefaultValidityLength);
            License.PaidFees = LicenseClass.ClassFees;
            License.Notes = txtNotes.Text.Trim();
            License.IsActive = true;
            License.IssueReason = 1; // 1 = First Time Issue
            License.CreatedByUserID = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.UserID;

            // 5. حفظ الرخصة وتحديث حالة الطلب
            if (License.Save())
            {
                _CreatedLicenseID = License.LicenseID;

                // استدعاء دالة Completed() المباشرة من الكلاس الخاص بك
                if (_LocalDrivingLicenseApplication.Completed())
                {
                    MessageBox.Show("License Issued Successfully with ID = " + _CreatedLicenseID, "Succeeded", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);

                    btnIssue.Enabled = false;
                    txtNotes.Enabled = false;
                    ucLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfo.EnableShowLicense = true;
                }
                else
                {
                    MessageBox.Show("License was created, but failed to update application status to Completed.", "Warning", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                }
            }
            else
            {
                MessageBox.Show("License Was Not Issued Successfully!", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            }
        }
    }

}﻿using DVLD_Business;
using System;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Tests
{
    public partial class frmScheduledTest : Form
    {
        private int _AppointmentID = -1;
        private int _TestTypeID = -1;
        private clsTestAppointmentBusiness? _Appointment;

        public frmScheduledTest(int AppointmentID, int TestTypeID)
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _AppointmentID = AppointmentID;
            _TestTypeID = TestTypeID;
        }

        private void frmScheduledTest_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _LoadData();
        }

        private void _LoadData()
        {
            _Appointment = clsTestAppointmentBusiness.GetTestAppointmentInfoByID(_AppointmentID);

            if (_Appointment == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Appointment not found.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                this.Close();
                return;
            }

            lblAnswerLocalApplicationID.Text = _Appointment.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID.ToString();
            lblAnswerDate.Text = _Appointment.AppointmentDate.ToShortDateString();
            lblAnswerFees.Text = _Appointment.PaidFees.ToString();
            lblAnswerTestID.Text = _Appointment.TestAppointmentID.ToString();

            clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness? LocalApp = clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness.GetLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfoByID(_Appointment.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID);

            if (LocalApp != null)
            {
                clsLicenseClassBusiness? LicenseClass = clsLicenseClassBusiness.GetLicenseClassInfoByID(LocalApp.LicenseClassID);
                if (LicenseClass != null)
                    lblAnswerClassName.Text = LicenseClass.ClassName;

                lblAnswerName.Text = LocalApp.ApplicationInfo.PersonInfo != null ? LocalApp.ApplicationInfo.PersonInfo.FullName : "Unknown Person";
            }

            int TotalTrials = clsTestAppointmentBusiness.GetTotalTrialsPerTest(_Appointment.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID, _TestTypeID);
            lblAnswerTrial.Text = (TotalTrials + 1).ToString();
        }

        private void btnSave_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_Appointment == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Appointment data not loaded.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            if (!rbPass.Checked && !rbFail.Checked)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please select a result (Pass or Fail).", "Validation Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            clsTestBusiness NewTest = new clsTestBusiness();
            NewTest.TestAppointmentID = _AppointmentID;
            NewTest.TestResult = rbPass.Checked;
            NewTest.Notes = txtNotes.Text.Trim();
            NewTest.CreatedByUserID = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.UserID;

            if (NewTest.AddNewTest())
            {
                _Appointment.LockTestAppointment();
                MessageBox.Show($"Test result saved successfully! Test ID = {NewTest.TestID}", "Success", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                this.DialogResult = DialogResult.OK;
                this.Close();
            }
            else
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Failed to save test result.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            }
        }
    }

    public partial class frmShowPersonLicenseHistory : Form
    {
        private int _PersonID = -1;

        public frmShowPersonLicenseHistory(int PersonID)
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _PersonID = PersonID;
        }

        private void frmShowPersonLicenseHistory_Load(object sender, EventArgs e) {

            if (_PersonID == -1) {
                MessageBox.Show("Error: Invalid Person ID.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                this.Close();
                return;
            }

            ucShowInfo.LoadPersonInfo(_PersonID);

            ucShowDriverLicensesHistory.LoadInfoByPersonID(_PersonID);



        }
    }
}

}﻿using DVLD_Business;
using DVLD_Presentation.User_Controls;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Tests
{
    public partial class frmTestAppointments : Form
    {

        private int _LocalAppID;
        private int _TestTypeID;
        private DataTable _dtTestAppointments;

        public frmTestAppointments(int LocalAppID, int TestTypeID) {
            InitializeComponent();
            _LocalAppID = LocalAppID;
            _TestTypeID = TestTypeID;
        }

        private void frmTestAppointments_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness? LocalApp = clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness.GetLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfoByID(_LocalAppID);

            ucLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfo.LoadLocalApplicationData(_LocalAppID);

            if (LocalApp != null)
                ucApplicationBasicInfo.LoadApplicationData(LocalApp.ApplicationID);


            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.BackColor = System.Drawing.ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#374151");
            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = Color.White;
            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionBackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316");
            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionForeColor = Color.White;

            clsFormatHelper.StyleDataGridView(dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications);
            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnAdd);

            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.AutoGenerateColumns = false;

            _RefreshTestAppointments();


            switch (_TestTypeID) {
                case 1:
                    lblTitleTestAppointments.Text = "Vision Test Appointments";
                    break;
                case 2:
                    lblTitleTestAppointments.Text = "Written Test Appointments";
                    break;
                case 3:
                    lblTitleTestAppointments.Text = "Street Test Appointments";
                    break;
            }

            this.Text = lblTitleTestAppointments.Text;

        }

        private void _RefreshTestAppointments() {
            _dtTestAppointments = clsTestAppointmentBusiness.GetAllTestAppointmentsByLocalAppID(_LocalAppID, _TestTypeID);
            dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.DataSource = _dtTestAppointments;
        }

        private void btnAdd_Click(object sender, EventArgs e) {

            frmAddUpdateTest frm = new frmAddUpdateTest(_LocalAppID, _TestTypeID);
            frm.ShowDialog();

            _RefreshTestAppointments();
        }

        private void itemTakeTest_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow != null && dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow.Cells[0].Value != null) {
                int AppointmentID = Convert.ToInt32(dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow.Cells[0].Value);

                frmScheduledTest frm = new frmScheduledTest(AppointmentID, _TestTypeID);
                frm.ShowDialog();

                _RefreshTestAppointments();
            }


        }

        private void itemEdit_Click(object sender, EventArgs e) {

            if (dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow == null || dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow.Cells[0].Value == null)
                return;

            int AppointmentID = Convert.ToInt32(dgvShowLocalDrivingLicenseApplications.CurrentRow.Cells[0].Value);

            frmAddUpdateTest frm = new frmAddUpdateTest(AppointmentID);
            frm.ShowDialog();

            _RefreshTestAppointments();

        }
    }
}

using DVLD_Presentation.People;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.User_Controls
{
    public partial class ucApplicationBasicInfo : UserControl
    {
        private clsApplicationBusiness? _Application;

        public ucApplicationBasicInfo()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        public void LoadApplicationData(int ApplicationID)
        {
            _Application = clsApplicationBusiness.GetApplicationInfoByID(ApplicationID);

            if (_Application == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("No Application with ID = " + ApplicationID.ToString(), "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            lblAnswerApplicationID.Text = _Application.ApplicationID.ToString();
            lblAnswerStatus.Text = _Application.ApplicationStatus.ToString();
            lblAnswerFees.Text = _Application.PaidFees.ToString();
            lblAnswerType.Text = _Application.ApplicationTypeID.ToString();

            lblAnswerDate.Text = _Application.ApplicationDate.ToShortDateString();
            lblAnswerStatusDate.Text = _Application.LastStatusDate.ToShortDateString();
            lblAnswerCreatedBy.Text = _Application.CreatedByUserID.ToString();

            clsPersonBusiness? Person = clsPersonBusiness.Find(_Application.ApplicantPersonID);

            lblAnswerApplicant.Text = _Application.PersonInfo?.FullName ?? "Unknown Person";

        }

        private void lnkViewPersonInfo_LinkClicked(object sender, LinkLabelLinkClickedEventArgs e) {

            frmShowPersonInfo frm = new frmShowPersonInfo(_Application.ApplicantPersonID);
            frm.ShowDialog();
            
        }
    }
}

﻿using DVLD_Business;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.User_Controls
{
    public partial class ucApplicationInfo : UserControl
    {

        private int _InternationalLicenseID = -1;
        private clsInternationalLicenseBusiness _InternationalLicense;

        public int InternationalLicenseID
        {
            get { return _InternationalLicenseID; }
        }

        public ucApplicationInfo()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        public void LoadApplicationInfo(clsInternationalLicenseBusiness InternationalLicense)
        {

            if (InternationalLicense == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("No International License information to display.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            _InternationalLicense = InternationalLicense;
            _InternationalLicenseID = InternationalLicense.InternationalLicenseID;

            lblAnswerInternationalLicenseApplicationID.Text = (InternationalLicense.ApplicationID == -1) ? "???" : InternationalLicense.ApplicationID.ToString();
            lblAnswerInternationalLicenseID.Text = (InternationalLicense.InternationalLicenseID == -1) ? "???" : InternationalLicense.InternationalLicenseID.ToString();
            lblAnswerLocalLicenseID.Text = InternationalLicense.IssuedUsingLocalLicenseID.ToString();
            lblAnswerIssueDate.Text = InternationalLicense.IssueDate.ToShortDateString();
            lblAnswerExpirationDate.Text = InternationalLicense.ExpirationDate.ToShortDateString();
            lblAnswerCreatedBy.Text = InternationalLicense.CreatedByUserID.ToString();


            if (InternationalLicense.ApplicationInfo != null)
            {
                lblAnswerApplicationDate.Text = InternationalLicense.ApplicationInfo.ApplicationDate.ToShortDateString();
                lblAnswerFees.Text = InternationalLicense.ApplicationInfo.PaidFees.ToString();
            }

        }



    }

﻿using DVLD_Business;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.User_Controls
{
    public partial class ucApplicationInfoForLicenseReplacement : UserControl {

        private clsLicenseBusiness? _OldLicense;

        public ucApplicationInfoForLicenseReplacement() {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        public void LoadData(int OldLicenseID, int ApplicationTypeID) {
            _OldLicense = clsLicenseBusiness.GetLicenseInfoByID(OldLicenseID);

            if (_OldLicense == null) {
                MessageBox.Show("Error: Could not find License with ID = " + OldLicenseID, "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            lblAnswerOldLicenseID.Text = _OldLicense.LicenseID.ToString();
            lblAnswerApplicationDate.Text = DateTime.Now.ToString("dd/MMM/yyyy");

            clsApplicationTypeBusiness? AppType = clsApplicationTypeBusiness.GetApplicationTypeInfoByID(ApplicationTypeID);
            
            if (AppType != null)
                lblAnswerApplicationFees.Text = AppType.ApplicationFees.ToString();

            lblAnswerCreatedBy.Text = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.Username;
            lblAnswerLicenseReplacementApplicationID.Text = "[???]";
            lblAnswerReplacedLicenseID.Text = "[???]";
        }

        private void ucApplicationInfoForLicenseReplacement_Load(object sender, EventArgs e) {

        }


        public void SetReplacementResult(int LicenseReplacementApplicationID, int ReplacedLicenseID) {
            lblAnswerLicenseReplacementApplicationID.Text = LicenseReplacementApplicationID.ToString();
            lblAnswerReplacedLicenseID.Text = ReplacedLicenseID.ToString();
        }

    }
}

﻿using DVLD_Business;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.User_Controls
{
    public partial class ucApplicationNewLicenseInfo : UserControl
    {
        private clsLicenseBusiness _OldLicense;

        public ucApplicationNewLicenseInfo()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        public string Notes
        {
            get { return txtNotes.Text.Trim(); }
        }

        public void LoadData(int OldLicenseID) {

            _OldLicense = clsLicenseBusiness.GetLicenseInfoByID(OldLicenseID);

            if (_OldLicense == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Error: Could not find License with ID = " + OldLicenseID, "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            lblAnswerOldLicenseID.Text = _OldLicense.LicenseID.ToString();
            lblAnswerApplicationDate.Text = DateTime.Now.ToString("dd/MMM/yyyy");
            lblAnswerIssueDate.Text = DateTime.Now.ToString("dd/MMM/yyyy");

            int DefaultValidityLength = _OldLicense.LicenseClassInfo.DefaultValidityLength;
            lblAnswerExpirationDate.Text = DateTime.Now.AddYears(DefaultValidityLength).ToString("dd/MMM/yyyy");

            clsApplicationTypeBusiness AppType = clsApplicationTypeBusiness.GetApplicationTypeInfoByID(2);
            if (AppType != null)
                lblAnswerApplicationFees.Text = AppType.ApplicationFees.ToString();


            lblAnswerLicenseFees.Text = _OldLicense.LicenseClassInfo.ClassFees.ToString();
            lblAnswerTotalFees.Text = (Convert.ToDecimal(lblAnswerApplicationFees.Text) + Convert.ToDecimal(lblAnswerLicenseFees.Text)).ToString();
            lblAnswerCreatedBy.Text = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.Username;
            lblAnswerRenewLicenseApplicationID.Text = "[???]";
            lblAnswerRenewedLicenseID.Text = "[???]";
        }

        public void SetRenewalResult(int RenewLicenseApplicationID, int RenewedLicenseID) {
            lblAnswerRenewLicenseApplicationID.Text = RenewLicenseApplicationID.ToString();
            lblAnswerRenewedLicenseID.Text = RenewedLicenseID.ToString();
        }




    }
}

﻿using DVLD_Business;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.User_Controls
{
    public partial class ucDetainInfo : UserControl
    {

        public decimal FineFees {
            get
            {
                decimal.TryParse(txtFineFees.Text.Trim(), out decimal Fees);
                return Fees;
            }
        }

        public ucDetainInfo() {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        public void LoadData()
        {
            lblAnswerCreatedBy.Text = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.Username;

            clsApplicationTypeBusiness? AppType = clsApplicationTypeBusiness.GetApplicationTypeInfoByID(5);
            if (AppType != null)
                lblAnswerApplicationFees.Text = AppType.ApplicationFees.ToString();

            lblAnswerDetainID.Text = "[???]";
            lblAnswerDetainDate.Text = "[???]";
        }

        public void SetDetainResult(int DetainID, DateTime DetainDate)
        {
            lblAnswerDetainID.Text = DetainID.ToString();
            lblAnswerDetainDate.Text = DetainDate.ToString("dd/MMM/yyyy");
        }




    }
}

    public partial class ucDriverInternationalLicenseInfo : UserControl

        private int _InternationalLicenseID = -1;

        }

        public void LoadInfo(int InternationalLicenseID)
        {
            _InternationalLicenseID = InternationalLicenseID;
            _InternationalLicense = clsInternationalLicenseBusiness.GetInternationalLicenseInfoByID(_InternationalLicenseID);

            if (_InternationalLicense == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show($"Could not find International License with ID = {_InternationalLicenseID}", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            lblAnswerInternationalLicenseID.Text = _InternationalLicense.InternationalLicenseID.ToString();
            lblAnswerApplicationID.Text = _InternationalLicense.ApplicationID.ToString();
            lblAnswerLicenseID.Text = _InternationalLicense.IssuedUsingLocalLicenseID.ToString();
            lblAnswerIsActive.Text = _InternationalLicense.IsActive ? "Yes" : "No";
            lblAnswerDriverID.Text = _InternationalLicense.DriverID.ToString();
            lblAnswerIssueDate.Text = _InternationalLicense.IssueDate.ToShortDateString();
            lblAnswerExpirationDate.Text = _InternationalLicense.ExpirationDate.ToShortDateString();

            if (_InternationalLicense.DriverInfo != null && _InternationalLicense.DriverInfo.PersonInfo != null)
            {
                lblAnswerName.Text = _InternationalLicense.DriverInfo.PersonInfo.FullName;
                lblAnswerNationalNo.Text = _InternationalLicense.DriverInfo.PersonInfo.NationalNo;
                lblAnswerDateOfBirth.Text = _InternationalLicense.DriverInfo.PersonInfo.DateOfBirth.ToShortDateString();
                lblAnswerGendor.Text = (_InternationalLicense.DriverInfo.PersonInfo.Gendor == 0 ? "Male" : "Female");
            }
            else
            {
                lblAnswerName.Text = "N/A";
                lblAnswerNationalNo.Text = "N/A";
                lblAnswerDateOfBirth.Text = "N/A";
                lblAnswerGendor.Text = "N/A";
            }
        }
    }

﻿using DVLD_Business;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.User_Controls
{
    public partial class ucDriverLicenseInfo : UserControl {

        private int _LicenseID = -1;
        private clsLicenseBusiness? _License;

        public int LicenseID {
            get { return _LicenseID; }
        }

        public clsLicenseBusiness? SelectedLicenseInfo
        {
            get { return _License; }
        }

        public ucDriverLicenseInfo() {
            InitializeComponent();
        }


        public void LoadInfo(int LicenseID) {

            _LicenseID = LicenseID;
            _License = clsLicenseBusiness.GetLicenseInfoByID(_LicenseID);

            if (_License == null) {
                MessageBox.Show($"Could not find License with ID = {_LicenseID}", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            lblAnswerLicenseID.Text = _License.LicenseID.ToString();
            lblAnswerDriverID.Text = _License.DriverID.ToString();
            lblAnswerIssueDate.Text = _License.IssueDate.ToShortDateString();
            lblAnswerExpirationDate.Text = _License.ExpirationDate.ToShortDateString();
            lblAnswerIssueReason.Text = _License.IssueReasonText;
            lblAnswerIsActive.Text = _License.IsActive ? "Yes" : "No";
            lblAnswerIsDetained.Text = "No"; // will be changed later

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_License.Notes))
                lblAnswerNotes.Text = "No Notes";
            else
                lblAnswerNotes.Text = _License.Notes;

            if (_License.LicenseClassInfo != null)
                lblAnswerClassName.Text = _License.LicenseClassInfo.ClassName;
            else
                lblAnswerClassName.Text = "N/A";

            if (_License.DriverInfo != null && _License.DriverInfo.PersonInfo != null)
            {
                lblAnswerName.Text = _License.DriverInfo.PersonInfo.FullName;
                lblAnswerNationalNo.Text = _License.DriverInfo.PersonInfo.NationalNo;
                lblAnswerDateOfBirth.Text = _License.DriverInfo.PersonInfo.DateOfBirth.ToShortDateString();

                lblAnswerGendor.Text = (_License.DriverInfo.PersonInfo.Gendor == 0 ? "Male" : "Female");

            }
            else
            {
                lblAnswerName.Text = "N/A";
                lblAnswerNationalNo.Text = "N/A";
                lblAnswerDateOfBirth.Text = "N/A";
                lblAnswerGendor.Text = "N/A";
            }

            _LoadPersonImage();

        }


        private void _LoadPersonImage()
        {
            if (_License == null || _License.DriverInfo == null || _License.DriverInfo.PersonInfo == null)
                return;


            string ImagePath = _License.DriverInfo.PersonInfo.ImagePath;

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(ImagePath))
            {

                 if (_License.DriverInfo.PersonInfo.Gendor == 0)
                    pbProfilePicture.Image = Properties.Resources.male;
                 else
                    pbProfilePicture.Image = Properties.Resources.female;
            }
            else if (File.Exists(ImagePath))
            {
                pbProfilePicture.ImageLocation = ImagePath;
            }
            else
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Could not find the person's image file.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
            }
        }



    }
}

﻿using DVLD_Business;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.User_Controls
{
    public partial class ucLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfo : UserControl
    {
        private clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness? _LocalApp;

        public bool EnableShowLicense {
            get { return lnkShowLicenseInfo.Enabled; }
            set { lnkShowLicenseInfo.Enabled = value; }
        }

        public ucLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfo() {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        public void LoadLocalApplicationData(int LocalAppID) {

            _LocalApp = clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness.GetLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfoByID(LocalAppID);

            if (_LocalApp == null) {
                MessageBox.Show("No Local Application with ID = " + LocalAppID.ToString(), "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            lblAnswerLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID.Text = _LocalApp.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID.ToString();


            int PassedTests = clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness.GetPassedTestsCount(LocalAppID);
            lblAnswerPassedTests.Text = PassedTests.ToString();

            clsLicenseClassBusiness? LicenseClass = clsLicenseClassBusiness.GetLicenseClassInfoByID(_LocalApp.LicenseClassID);

            if (LicenseClass != null)
                lblAnswerLicenseClass.Text = LicenseClass.ClassName;
            

        }



    }
}

﻿using DVLD_Business;
using System;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.User_Controls
{
    public partial class ucReleaseInfo : UserControl
    {
        private clsDetainedLicenseBusiness? _Detain;

        public ucReleaseInfo() {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        public clsDetainedLicenseBusiness? SelectedDetainInfo {
            get { return _Detain; }
        }

        public void LoadData(int LicenseID) {
            _Detain = clsDetainedLicenseBusiness.GetActiveDetainInfoByLicenseID(LicenseID);

            if (_Detain == null) {
                MessageBox.Show("Error: Could not find an active Detain record for License ID = " + LicenseID, "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            lblAnswerDetainID.Text = _Detain.DetainID.ToString();
            lblAnswerLicenseID.Text = _Detain.LicenseID.ToString();
            lblAnswerDetainDate.Text = _Detain.DetainDate.ToString("dd/MMM/yyyy");
            lblAnswerCreatedBy.Text = clsGlobal.CurrentUser.Username;
            lblAnswerFineFees.Text = _Detain.FineFees.ToString();

            clsApplicationTypeBusiness? AppType = clsApplicationTypeBusiness.GetApplicationTypeInfoByID(5);
            decimal ApplicationFees = (AppType != null) ? AppType.ApplicationFees : 0;
            lblAnswerApplicationFees.Text = ApplicationFees.ToString();

            lblAnswerTotalFees.Text = (ApplicationFees + _Detain.FineFees).ToString();

            lblAnswerApplicationID.Text = "[???]";
        }

        public void SetReleaseResult(int ReleaseApplicationID) {
            lblAnswerApplicationID.Text = ReleaseApplicationID.ToString();
        }
    }

﻿using DVLD_Business;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation
{
    public partial class ucShowAllInfo : UserControl
    {
        public ucShowAllInfo()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void pnlTextBorder_Paint(object sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Panel pnl = (Panel)sender;
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;

            using (Pen pen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316"), 2))
            {
                Rectangle rect = new Rectangle(0, 0, pnl.Width - 1, pnl.Height - 1);
                using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = clsFormatHelper.GetRoundedRectanglePath(rect, 15))
                {

                    using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937")))
                    {
                        e.Graphics.FillPath(brush, path);
                    }
                    e.Graphics.DrawPath(pen, path);
                }
            }
        }

        private void ucShowInfo_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            clsFormatHelper.MakePictureBoxCircular(pbProfilePicture);
        }

        public void LoadUserInfo(clsUserBusiness user) {

            lblAnswerUserID.Text = user.UserID.ToString();
            lblAnswerPersonID.Text = user.PersonID.ToString();
            lblAnswerUsername.Text = user.Username;
            lblAnswerIsActive.Text = user.IsActive ? "Yes" : "No";

            clsPersonBusiness? person = clsPersonBusiness.Find(user.PersonID);

            if (person != null) {
                lblName.Text = person.FirstName + " " + person.SecondName + " " + person.ThirdName + " " + person.LastName;
                lblAnswerNationalNo.Text = person.NationalNo;
                lblAnswerDateOfBirth.Text = person.DateOfBirth.ToShortDateString();
                lblAnswerGender.Text = (person.Gendor == 0) ? "Male" : "Female";
                lblAnswerCountry.Text = clsPersonBusiness.GetCountryNameByID(person.NationalityCountryID);

                lblAnswerPhone.Text = person.Phone;
                lblAnswerEmail.Text = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(person.Email) ? "N/A" : person.Email;
                lblAnswerAddress.Text = person.Address;

                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(person.ImagePath) && System.IO.File.Exists(person.ImagePath))
                    pbProfilePicture.Load(person.ImagePath);
                else {
                    if (person.Gendor == 0)
                        pbProfilePicture.Image = Properties.Resources.male; 
                    else
                        pbProfilePicture.Image = Properties.Resources.female; 
                }


            }
        }


    }
}

namespace DVLD_Presentation.User_Controls
{
    public partial class ucShowDriverLicensesHistory : UserControl
    {
        private int _DriverID = -1;
        private DataTable _dtDriverLocalLicensesHistory;
        private DataTable _dtDriverInternationalLicensesHistory; // Added DataTable for International

        public ucShowDriverLicensesHistory()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void ucShowDriverLicensesHistory_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            dgvShowDriverLocalLicensesHistory.AutoGenerateColumns = false;
            dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.AutoGenerateColumns = false; // Disabled for International
        }

        public void LoadInfoByPersonID(int PersonID)
        {
            clsDriverBusiness? Driver = clsDriverBusiness.GetDriverInfoByPersonID(PersonID);

            if (Driver != null)
            {
                _DriverID = Driver.DriverID;

                // Load both grids
                _LoadLocalLicensesInfo();
                _LoadInternationalLicensesInfo();
            }
            else
            {
                // Clear both grids if driver not found
                _dtDriverLocalLicensesHistory = null;
                dgvShowDriverLocalLicensesHistory.DataSource = _dtDriverLocalLicensesHistory;

                _dtDriverInternationalLicensesHistory = null;
                dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.DataSource = _dtDriverInternationalLicensesHistory;
            }
        }

        private void _LoadLocalLicensesInfo()
        {
            _dtDriverLocalLicensesHistory = clsDriverBusiness.GetLocalLicenses(_DriverID);
            dgvShowDriverLocalLicensesHistory.DataSource = _dtDriverLocalLicensesHistory;

            if (_dtDriverLocalLicensesHistory != null && _dtDriverLocalLicensesHistory.Rows.Count > 0)
            {
                dgvShowDriverLocalLicensesHistory.Columns["LicenseID"].FillWeight = 10;
                dgvShowDriverLocalLicensesHistory.Columns["ApplicationID"].FillWeight = 10;
                dgvShowDriverLocalLicensesHistory.Columns["ClassName"].FillWeight = 34;
                dgvShowDriverLocalLicensesHistory.Columns["IssueDate"].FillWeight = 18;
                dgvShowDriverLocalLicensesHistory.Columns["ExpirationDate"].FillWeight = 18;
                dgvShowDriverLocalLicensesHistory.Columns["IsActive"].FillWeight = 10;
            }

            dgvShowDriverLocalLicensesHistory.BackColor = System.Drawing.ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            dgvShowDriverLocalLicensesHistory.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#374151");
            dgvShowDriverLocalLicensesHistory.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = Color.White;
            dgvShowDriverLocalLicensesHistory.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionBackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316");
            dgvShowDriverLocalLicensesHistory.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionForeColor = Color.White;

            clsFormatHelper.StyleDataGridView(dgvShowDriverLocalLicensesHistory);
        }

        // Added method to load and style the International Licenses DataGridView
        private void _LoadInternationalLicensesInfo()
        {
            _dtDriverInternationalLicensesHistory = clsDriverBusiness.GetInternationalLicenses(_DriverID);
            dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.DataSource = _dtDriverInternationalLicensesHistory;

            if (_dtDriverInternationalLicensesHistory != null && _dtDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.Rows.Count > 0)
            {
                // Note: These names exactly match the Name properties in your designer code
                dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.Columns["InternationalLicenseID"].FillWeight = 16;
                dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.Columns["ApplicationIDInternatonal"].FillWeight = 16;
                dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.Columns["LocalLicenseID"].FillWeight = 16;
                dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.Columns["IssueDateInternational"].FillWeight = 18;
                dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.Columns["ExpirationDateInternational"].FillWeight = 18;
                dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.Columns["IsActiveInternational"].FillWeight = 16;
            }

            dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.BackColor = System.Drawing.ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937");
            dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#374151");
            dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = Color.White;
            dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionBackColor = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316");
            dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionForeColor = Color.White;

            clsFormatHelper.StyleDataGridView(dgvShowDriverInternationalLicensesHistory);
        }
    }

﻿using DVLD_Business;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation
{
    public partial class ucShowInfo : UserControl
    {

        public int PersonID { get; private set; } = -1;

        public ucShowInfo()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void pnlTextBorder_Paint(object sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Panel pnl = (Panel)sender;
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;

            using (Pen pen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316"), 2))
            {
                Rectangle rect = new Rectangle(0, 0, pnl.Width - 1, pnl.Height - 1);
                using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = clsFormatHelper.GetRoundedRectanglePath(rect, 15))
                {

                    using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937")))
                    {
                        e.Graphics.FillPath(brush, path);
                    }
                    e.Graphics.DrawPath(pen, path);
                }
            }
        }

        private void ucShowInfo_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            clsFormatHelper.MakePictureBoxCircular(pbProfilePicture);


        }

        public void LoadPersonInfo(string NationalNo) {
            clsPersonBusiness? PersonInfo = clsPersonBusiness.Find(NationalNo); ;

            if (PersonInfo != null)
            {
                this.PersonID = PersonInfo.PersonID;

                lblAnswerGender.Text = (PersonInfo.Gendor == 0 ? "Male" : "Female");
                lblAnswerEmail.Text = PersonInfo.Email;
                lblAnswerNationalNo.Text = PersonInfo.NationalNo;
                lblAnswerPhone.Text = PersonInfo.Phone;
                lblAnswerCountry.Text = clsPersonBusiness.GetCountryNameByID(PersonInfo.NationalityCountryID); ;
                lblAnswerDateOfBirth.Text = PersonInfo.DateOfBirth.ToShortDateString();
                lblName.Text = PersonInfo.FirstName + " " + PersonInfo.LastName;

                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(PersonInfo.ImagePath) && System.IO.File.Exists(PersonInfo.ImagePath))
                    pbProfilePicture.ImageLocation = PersonInfo.ImagePath;
                else
                {
                    if (PersonInfo.Gendor == 0)
                        pbProfilePicture.Image = Properties.Resources.male;
                    else
                        pbProfilePicture.Image = Properties.Resources.female;
                }
            }
            else
                this.PersonID = -1;
        }


        public void LoadPersonInfo(int PersonID) {
            clsPersonBusiness? PersonInfo = clsPersonBusiness.Find(PersonID);

            if (PersonInfo != null)
            {
                this.PersonID = PersonInfo.PersonID;

                lblAnswerGender.Text = (PersonInfo.Gendor == 0 ? "Male" : "Female");
                lblAnswerEmail.Text = PersonInfo.Email;
                lblAnswerNationalNo.Text = PersonInfo.NationalNo;
                lblAnswerPhone.Text = PersonInfo.Phone;
                lblAnswerCountry.Text = clsPersonBusiness.GetCountryNameByID(PersonInfo.NationalityCountryID); ;
                lblAnswerDateOfBirth.Text = PersonInfo.DateOfBirth.ToShortDateString();
                lblName.Text = PersonInfo.FirstName + " " + PersonInfo.LastName;

                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(PersonInfo.ImagePath) && System.IO.File.Exists(PersonInfo.ImagePath))
                    pbProfilePicture.ImageLocation = PersonInfo.ImagePath;
                else {
                    if (PersonInfo.Gendor == 0)
                        pbProfilePicture.Image = Properties.Resources.male;
                    else
                        pbProfilePicture.Image = Properties.Resources.female;
                }
            }
            else
                this.PersonID = -1;

        }

    }
}

﻿using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using DVLD_Business;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.User_Controls
{
    public partial class ucShowTestInfo : UserControl {

        private clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness? _LocalAppInfo;
        private int _TestTypeID = -1;

        public DateTimePicker TestDatePicker
        {
            get
            {
                return timeTestDate;
            }
        }

        public ucShowTestInfo() {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        public int LoadData(int LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID, int TestTypeID) {

            _TestTypeID = TestTypeID;

            _LocalAppInfo = clsLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationBusiness.GetLocalDrivingLicenseApplicationInfoByID(LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID);

            if (_LocalAppInfo == null) {
                MessageBox.Show("Error: Local Application not found!", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return -1;
            }

            lblAnswerLocalAppID.Text = _LocalAppInfo.LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID.ToString();

            clsLicenseClassBusiness? LicenseClass = clsLicenseClassBusiness.GetLicenseClassInfoByID(_LocalAppInfo.LicenseClassID);
            lblAnswerClassName.Text = LicenseClass != null ? LicenseClass.ClassName : "Unknown";

            lblAnswerName.Text = _LocalAppInfo.ApplicationInfo.PersonInfo != null ? _LocalAppInfo.ApplicationInfo.PersonInfo.FullName : "Unknown Person";

            int TrialCount = clsTestAppointmentBusiness.GetTotalTrialsPerTest(LocalDrivingLicenseApplicationID, _TestTypeID);
            lblAnswerTrial.Text = TrialCount.ToString();

            clsTestTypeBusiness? TestType = clsTestTypeBusiness.GetTestTypeInfoByID(_TestTypeID);
            if (TestType != null)
                lblAnswerFees.Text = TestType.TestTypeFees.ToString("0.00");

            return TrialCount;

        }



    }
}

﻿using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using DVLD_Business;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Users
{
    public partial class frmAddUpdateUser : Form
    {

        public frmAddUpdateUser()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void frmAddUpdateUser_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnNext);
            clsFormatHelper.StyleButton(btnSearch);
        }

        private void btnSearch_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (cmbFilterBy.SelectedItem == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please select a filter category first.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtSearchUser.Text))
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please enter a value to search.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            string SelectedItem = cmbFilterBy.SelectedItem.ToString();
            string SearchValue = txtSearchUser.Text.Trim();

            if (SelectedItem == "Person ID")
            {
                if (int.TryParse(SearchValue, out int PersonID))
                    ucShowPersonInfo.LoadPersonInfo(PersonID);
                else
                    MessageBox.Show("Please enter a valid numeric Person ID.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            }
            else if (SelectedItem == "National No.")
                ucShowPersonInfo.LoadPersonInfo(SearchValue);
        }

        private void btnNext_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (ucShowPersonInfo.PersonID == -1)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please select a person first.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            frmAddUserInfo frm = new frmAddUserInfo(ucShowPersonInfo.PersonID);
            frm.ShowDialog();
        }

        private void pnlTextBorder_Paint(object sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Panel pnl = (Panel)sender;
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;

            using (Pen pen = new Pen(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#f97316"), 2))
            {
                Rectangle rect = new Rectangle(0, 0, pnl.Width - 1, pnl.Height - 1);
                using (System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath path = clsFormatHelper.GetRoundedRectanglePath(rect, 15))
                {
                    using (SolidBrush brush = new SolidBrush(ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#1f2937")))
                    {
                        e.Graphics.FillPath(brush, path);
                    }
                    e.Graphics.DrawPath(pen, path);
                }
            }
        }

        private void txtSearchUser_TextChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
        }

        private void frmAddUpdateUser_Load_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }
    }

}﻿using DVLD_Business;
using DVLD_Presentation.People;
using System;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Users
{
    public partial class frmAddUserInfo : Form
    {
        private int _PersonID;
        private clsUserBusiness? _User;


        public frmAddUserInfo(int PersonID)
        {
            InitializeComponent(); 
            _PersonID = PersonID;
        }

        private void frmAddUserInfo_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _LoadData();
            lnkUpdatePerson.Visible = (_PersonID != -1);
        }

        private void _LoadData() {
            
            _User = clsUserBusiness.FindByPersonID(_PersonID);

            if (_User != null)
            {

                txtUsername.Text = _User.Username;
                txtPassword.Text = _User.Password;
                txtConfirmPassword.Text = _User.Password;
                chkIsActive.Checked = _User.IsActive;
            }
            else
            {
                _User = new clsUserBusiness();
                _User.PersonID = _PersonID;

                txtUsername.Text = string.Empty;
                txtPassword.Text = string.Empty;
                txtConfirmPassword.Text = string.Empty;
                chkIsActive.Checked = true;
            }
        }


        private void btnSave_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_PersonID <= 0)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please select a person first.", "Error",
                    MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtUsername.Text) ||
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtPassword.Text))
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Username and Password are required!", "Validation Error",
                    MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            if (txtPassword.Text.Trim() != txtConfirmPassword.Text.Trim())
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Password and Confirm Password do not match!", "Validation Error",
                    MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            _User.Username = txtUsername.Text.Trim();
            _User.Password = txtPassword.Text.Trim();
            _User.IsActive = chkIsActive.Checked;

            if (_User.Save())
            {
                MessageBox.Show($"User Saved Successfully! User ID = {_User.UserID}", "Success",
                    MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                this.DialogResult = DialogResult.OK;
                this.Close();
            }
            else
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Failed to save user data. Please check if username is duplicated.",
                    "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            }
        }

        private void linkUpdatePerson_LinkClicked(object sender, LinkLabelLinkClickedEventArgs e)
        {
            frmAddUpdatePerson frm = new frmAddUpdatePerson(_PersonID);
            frm.ShowDialog();
        }
    }

}﻿using DVLD_Business;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Users
{
    public partial class frmChangePassword : Form
    {
        private int _UserID;
        private clsUserBusiness? _User;

        public frmChangePassword(int UserID)
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _UserID = UserID;
        }

        private void frmChangePassword_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _User = clsUserBusiness.FindByUserID(_UserID);

            if (_User == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("User not found.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                this.Close();
            }
        }

        private void _PasswordFields_TextChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(txtConfirmPassword.Text))
            {
                errPasswordMatch.SetError(txtConfirmPassword, "");
                return;
            }

            if (txtNewPassword.Text != txtConfirmPassword.Text)
                errPasswordMatch.SetError(txtConfirmPassword, "Passwords do not match!");
            else
                errPasswordMatch.SetError(txtConfirmPassword, "");
        }

        private void btnSave_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtOldPassword.Text) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtNewPassword.Text) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtConfirmPassword.Text)) {
                MessageBox.Show("Please fill all required password fields!", "Validation Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            if (_User.Password != txtOldPassword.Text) {
                MessageBox.Show("Current password is incorrect!", "Wrong Password", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            if (txtNewPassword.Text != txtConfirmPassword.Text) {
                errPasswordMatch.SetError(txtConfirmPassword, "Passwords do not match!");
                MessageBox.Show("New password and confirm password do not match!", "Validation Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            _User.Password = txtNewPassword.Text;

            if (_User.Save()) {
                MessageBox.Show("Password Changed Successfully.", "Success", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                this.Close(); 
            }
            else
                MessageBox.Show("Failed to change password.", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);

        }
    }
}

    partial class frmChangePassword
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Required designer variable.
        /// </summary>
        private System.ComponentModel.IContainer components = null;

        /// <summary>

        {
            if (disposing && (components != null))
            {
                components.Dispose();

            base.Dispose(disposing);
        }

        #region Windows Form Designer generated code

        /// <summary>
        /// Required method for Designer support - do not modify
        /// the contents of this method with the code editor.
        /// </summary>
        private void InitializeComponent()
        {
            components = new System.ComponentModel.Container();
            txtOldPassword = new Sunny.UI.UITextBox();
            txtNewPassword = new Sunny.UI.UITextBox();
            txtConfirmPassword = new Sunny.UI.UITextBox();
            lblOldPassword = new Sunny.UI.UILabel();
            lblConfirmPassword = new Sunny.UI.UILabel();
            lblNewPassword = new Sunny.UI.UILabel();
            btnSave = new Button();
            lblChangePassword = new Sunny.UI.UILabel();
            errPasswordMatch = new ErrorProvider(components);
            ((System.ComponentModel.ISupportInitialize)errPasswordMatch).BeginInit();
            SuspendLayout();
            // 
            // txtOldPassword
            // 
            txtOldPassword.FillColor = Color.FromArgb(55, 65, 81);
            txtOldPassword.Font = new Font("Microsoft Sans Serif", 12F);
            txtOldPassword.ForeColor = Color.White;
            txtOldPassword.Location = new Point(181, 51);
            txtOldPassword.Margin = new Padding(4, 5, 4, 5);
            txtOldPassword.MinimumSize = new Size(1, 16);
            txtOldPassword.Name = "txtOldPassword";
            txtOldPassword.Padding = new Padding(5);
            txtOldPassword.RectColor = Color.FromArgb(47, 59, 73);
            txtOldPassword.ScrollBarColor = Color.FromArgb(47, 59, 73);

}﻿using DVLD_Business;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;

using System.Linq;

using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using static System.Windows.Forms.VisualStyles.VisualStyleElement.StartPanel;

namespace DVLD_Presentation.Users
{
    public partial class frmLogin : Form
    {
        public frmLogin()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void btnLogin_Click(object sender, EventArgs e) {

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtUsername.Text) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txtPassword.Text)) {
                MessageBox.Show("Empty fields!", "Invalid Login", MessageBoxButtons.RetryCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            clsUserBusiness? user = clsUserBusiness.FindByUsernameAndPassword(txtUsername.Text, txtPassword.Text);

            if (user == null) {
                MessageBox.Show("Invalid Username or Password!", "Invalid Login", MessageBoxButtons.RetryCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            if (user.IsActive == false) {
                MessageBox.Show("Your account is not active, please contact your admin.", "Invalid Login", MessageBoxButtons.RetryCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return;
            }

            clsGlobal.CurrentUser = user;
            this.Hide();
            frmMainMenu frm = new frmMainMenu();
            frm.Show();

        }
    }
}
